Add repeated node birth/deletion sequences and test duplicate-mint/idempotence separately on full CAN closure rules containing can_native_note_coactivation (AddNode). Then validate native effect-to-frame notifications against full rescans for outputs, fixed-point residual, progress accounting, frame visits, candidate work, batching, partition maintenance, and memory. No general learned frame hierarchy or natural-task cheap-cut evidence is established. A post-hoc boundary analysis over the 78 selected Bigville/conversation/discourse snapshots finds median distinct external graph-node boundaries of 2 for five-node Bigville frames, 27 for nine-node conversation frames, and 19 for eleven-node discourse frames; Bigville has a 100-node maximum and all families have unassigned-endpoint incidence. These are runtime-labeled state-graph neighbors, not factor interfaces or natural-prevalence estimates ([boundary results](SUBSTRATE_TASK_FRAME_BOUNDARY_SIZES_POSTHOC_V1_RESULTS_20261005.md), [analysis](../../../evaluation/reports/task_frame_boundary_sizes_posthoc_v1_20261005.json)). |
| Structural width, frame cuts, and hierarchy | `evaluation/substrate_capability/llm_transport_varied_topology_solver_v1_3.py::worker`, `evaluation/substrate_capability/analyze_llm_transport_varied_topology_solver_v1_3.py::main`, `domains/graph_treewidth.py::exact_treewidth`, `domains/graph_treewidth.py::min_fill_upper_bound`, `domains/graph_treewidth.py::treewidth_bound_check`, `domains/graph_treewidth.py::build_elimination_triangulation`, `domains/can_junction_tree.py::junction_tree_exact_settle`, `domains/problem_decomposition.py::decompose_problem`, `domains/solver_dispatch.py::solve_decompose`, `domains/solver_dispatch.py::mint_solve_decompose_goal_from_text`, `scripts/graph_treewidth_cost_envelope.py::main`, `scripts/substrate_structural_census_pilot.py::recursive_partition`, `scripts/substrate_rule_dependency_census.py::native_access_audit` | Offline census routines operate on copied Python node/edge lists and do not partition native rule execution. Selected solver callers invoke bounded-width gates; the opt-in decomposition resolver returns a decomposition/refusal report and may mint projection-race bookkeeping nodes. | **Structural mechanism demonstrated on synthetic and source-derived workload graphs; analysis cost measured on synthetic families.** [`test_solver_decompose_adapter.py`](../../../tests/test_solver_decompose_adapter.py) and [`test_solver_dispatch_migrated.py`](../../../tests/test_solver_dispatch_migrated.py) cover selected solver paths. Exact treewidth on six synthetic n=8 graphs; min-fill bounds through n=64; 144 synthetic partitions. A current-source cost envelope adds repeated exact timings through n=10 and min-fill timings through n=512 on paths, bands, sparse-random, and complete graphs. Exact median is 3.6–4.9 s at n=10, while min-fill remains below 0.6 s through n=512; returned order widths independently reconcile ([`SUBSTRATE_TREEWIDTH_COST_ENVELOPE_RESULTS.md`](SUBSTRATE_TREEWIDTH_COST_ENVELOPE_RESULTS.md), [raw report](../../../evaluation/reports/graph_treewidth_cost_envelope_20260925.json)). A current-source three-worker replay runs the existing 96 shared-separator CAN models (288 captures) and independently confirms exact MAP agreement, model extraction, clique width, and `F·d^(k+m)` local assignment/factor-touch counts. This supports the explicit bounded-interior proposition, while cuts remain supplied by construction and inference is Python-side ([proposition](SUBSTRATE_SEPARATOR_FACTORISATION_COST_PROPOSITION_20260926.md), [protocol](SUBSTRATE_CAN_SEPARATOR_FACTORIZATION_REPLAY_PREREG_20260926.md), [results](SUBSTRATE_SEPARATOR_FACTORISATION_COST_PROPOSITION_20260926.md), [analysis](../../../evaluation/reports/can_separator_factorization_replay_v1_analysis_20260926.json)). A current-source Bigville aggregate inference run discovers disconnected components from the extracted 100-resident/500-Concept causal graph without resident labels. Whole-graph min-fill/JT and component-local exhaustive MAP agree exactly; at 100 residents the respective inference timings are 85.45 ms and 59.31 ms including component discovery. This is an authored disconnected positive control, not connected-boundary factorization ([protocol](SUBSTRATE_BIGVILLE_AGGREGATE_COMPONENT_INFERENCE_PREREG_20260926.md), [results](SUBSTRATE_BIGVILLE_AGGREGATE_COMPONENT_INFERENCE_V1_RESULTS_20260926.md), [analysis](../../../evaluation/reports/substrate_bigville_aggregate_component_inference_v1_analysis_20260926.json)). A preregistered binary ladder inference sweep extends exact MAP from width one to width two through 1,024 variables: all 18 cases match an independent rung-DP oracle, while min-fill ordering reaches 440 ms at the largest point ([ladder results](CAN_WIDTH_TWO_LADDER_SCALE_RESULTS.md), [raw](../../../evaluation/reports/can_width_two_ladder_scale_20260925.json), [lock](../../../evaluation/substrate_capability/can_width_two_ladder_scale_lock_20260925.json)). A 60-case strip-width profile across heights 1–5 passes extraction/oracle checks; column-order certificates expose a one-unit min-fill overestimate for height 5 at length 32 and above ([profile results](CAN_STRIP_WIDTH_PROFILE_RESULTS.md), [raw](../../../evaluation/reports/can_strip_width_profile_v1_1_20260925.json), [lock](../../../evaluation/substrate_capability/can_strip_width_profile_v1_1_lock_20260925.json)). Source-derived projections cover CAN and conversation deliberation with global-key vs role-refined alias assumptions; see [`SUBSTRATE_STRUCTURAL_CENSUS_PILOT_RESULTS.md`](SUBSTRATE_STRUCTURAL_CENSUS_PILOT_RESULTS.md) and [`SUBSTRATE_RULE_DEPENDENCY_CENSUS_RESULTS.md`](SUBSTRATE_RULE_DEPENDENCY_CENSUS_RESULTS.md). A full-corpus native access census adds 2,289 active rules: global `Attr` keys create 613,049 directed dependencies, with `attr:status` alone contributing 520,322 possible writer-reader pairs; 96.6% of edges cross RuleIdx blocks of 32. Wildcard completion joins all rules into one SCC. Since bound-node identity is absent and this is a static authored rule library, it warns against treating this projection as evidence for cheap cuts but does not refute task-level locality ([full census results](SUBSTRATE_FULL_RULE_DEPENDENCY_CENSUS_RESULTS.md), [protocol](SUBSTRATE_FULL_RULE_DEPENDENCY_CENSUS_PREREG_20260925.md), [raw](../../../evaluation/reports/substrate_full_rule_dependency_census_v1_20260925.json), [analysis](../../../evaluation/reports/substrate_full_rule_dependency_census_v1_analysis_20260925.json), [runner](../../../scripts/substrate_full_rule_dependency_census_v1.py), [lock](../../../evaluation/substrate_capability/full_rule_dependency_census_lock_20260925.json)). A static source census of the authored `vision_settle` seed finds its batched SUnit update reads both local neighbors and a global `NodesOfType(SUnit)` mean; the latter implies K_N dependency width N−1. For N=64 and four-unit frames, 60 external activations per frame and 93.75% global input incidences cross frame cuts; this is symbolic, not runtime cost. The current scoped runner accepts Microtheory scopes through `has_concept`, not arbitrary SUnit frames ([`SUBSTRATE_VISION_SETTLE_CHEAP_CUT_CENSUS_RESULTS.md`](SUBSTRATE_VISION_SETTLE_CHEAP_CUT_CENSUS_RESULTS.md)). A preregistered native two-sheet probe confirms that changing B from `[0,0]` to `[1,1]` changes sheet A's `st_settle` output from `[0.5,0.5]` to `[0,0]`; `NodesOfType(SUnit)` crosses separate World roots in one substrate. The v1/v1.1 fixture failures are retained; v1.2 passed the independent analyzer. This is a minimal semantic probe, not a full sheet or cost run ([`SUBSTRATE_VISION_SETTLE_MULTI_SHEET_PROBE_RESULTS.md`](SUBSTRATE_VISION_SETTLE_MULTI_SHEET_PROBE_RESULTS.md)).  See the cross-artifact [Phase C gate audit](SUBSTRATE_CHEAP_CUT_GATE_AUDIT_20260926.md): C1/C2/C2a/C3 are separately graded; cheap cuts remain workload-specific and general boundary-aware factorisation is unestablished. A post-hoc profile of 63 runtime-derived Bigville checkpoints measures changing owner-frame boundaries and quotient width bounds: the star control has one exposed Concept per active frame and width upper bound 1, yet center fan-out reaches 99; caller edge/list mutations total about 20 ms per seven-checkpoint sequence, while partition construction/update timing remains missing ([results](SUBSTRATE_BIGVILLE_DYNAMIC_BOUNDARY_PROFILE_RESULTS_20260926.md), [analysis](../../../evaluation/reports/substrate_bigville_dynamic_boundary_profile_v1_20260926.json)). **Runtime-derived scale:** 48 workers at N=32–256 on settled path/banded2 CAN networks match exact JT against independent VE, with width 1/2 and near-linear JT/allocation scaling (8x N costs 7.7–8.6x JT time). A paired 16-worker audit attributes superlinear closure work in this fixture: doubling N from 128 to 256 raises candidate pairs 10.4–10.8x; Hebbian mint/reweight and coactivation produce 99.96% of Term evaluations, with audited/uninstrumented state parity. Fixture-generated, not a natural graph distribution. A current-source 21-turn native access capture adds owned/external attribute schemas and event-level scopes to the held-topic runtime cuts. Trace integrity and cross-worker structural repeat pass, but the task-quality gate fails on one held copper prompt in all three workers. Frames contain 10–13 owned nodes and 40–51 boundary incidences while 112k–309k observed native access events per turn touch nodes outside the direct cut; this measures projection coverage, not cut sufficiency or safe partitioning. Ownership remains protocol-declared and host/scheduler trace coverage is incomplete ([results](SUBSTRATE_HELD_TOPIC_RUNTIME_INTERFACE_TRACE_V1_RESULTS_20261001.md), [protocol](SUBSTRATE_HELD_TOPIC_RUNTIME_INTERFACE_TRACE_V1_PREREG_20261001.md), [analysis](../../../evaluation/reports/held_topic_runtime_interface_trace_v1_20261001/analysis_v1_1.json), [capture](../../../evaluation/reports/held_topic_runtime_interface_trace_v1_20261001/capture.json.gz)). A 2026-10-03 three-worker exact solver panel covers 320 unique generated 16-node factor graphs per worker. All energy/sign/direct-rescore gates pass; the full batch is 1.95–1.99× faster than the current Python JT baseline, with fan-in-specific ratios from 0.32× to 2.27×. It is not natural-task prevalence or native frame scheduling evidence ([results](LLM_TRANSPORT_VARIED_TOPOLOGY_SOLVER_V1_3_RESULTS_20261003.md), [preregistration](LLM_TRANSPORT_VARIED_TOPOLOGY_SOLVER_V1_PREREG_20261003.md), [source lock](../../../evaluation/substrate_capability/llm_transport_varied_topology_solver_v1_3_lock_20261003.json), [capture](../../../evaluation/reports/llm_transport_varied_topology_solver_v1_3_20261003.json), [analysis](../../../evaluation/reports/llm_transport_varied_topology_solver_v1_3_analysis_20261003.json)). | The conversation seed's exact width changes from 9 to 4 depending on attribute alias modeling, so dependency completeness is unresolved. The role-refined projection may omit dependencies. Width-analysis cost does not include graph extraction, triangulation/message passing, or memory profiling. The exact global dependency result for `vision_settle` is a concrete exception to small separators; the term is not scoped to a World and may couple multiple sheets in one substrate (not yet runtime-tested). No generic SUnit frame execution API, live locality distribution, dynamic update cost, or partitioned-versus-monolithic spatial result exists. Cross-World coupling is now verified on a minimal two-pair fixture. A benchmark-only clone using `Neighbours(r, has_sunit)` expresses root-scoped competition in the DSL and removes B-to-A dependence on that fixture; v1 had a failed numeric prediction, retained, while corrected v1.1 passes independent arithmetic/provenance audit. A four-pair 3×3/4×4 `build_sheet` comparison then isolates native settle/commit rules: global and Retina-root-scoped variants have identical terminal states and progress counts; independent equation residuals are ≤2.23e-16. Three cases hit the exact 10,000-application cap from last-bit dither, so report numerical convergence separately from engine termination. A four-phase orientation pilot then showed exact tuning/OSI parity on two sheets, but both mean OSI values were low (0.0132, 0.0176) and no weights were developed. The stock presenter’s host writes followed by default `tick` produced zero progress and zero tuning; full-rescan closure on the same stimulus activated units. Thus the amended full-rescan comparison is pilot evidence only, and the enabled DualProcess policy routes default tick to `hard_realtime` while vision rules are untagged; this is a plan/rule-class mismatch, not a demonstrated notification defect. A benchmark-only tag gate assigned the five vision families `hard_realtime`: default tick made 111 applications and exactly matched full-rescan state on one frame. A corrected one-seed K=16 orientation run then found exact tuning parity across global/root-scoped and full-rescan/tagged-tick variants after retinal development and theta adaptation; mean OSI was 0.252461 in all arms. This is baseline response parity, not learned selectivity. The raw v1.1 runner mislabels positive tick counts as cycle detection and omits exact per-call progress; termination reason is unavailable. One confounded v1 partial was overwritten and is explicitly excluded. A same-seed acute settle-on/off K=16 ablation then found exact tuning parity (mean OSI 0.294689) but 91,006 versus 2,848 progress applications over 480 probe presentations, a 96.87% reduction when settling was disabled. One seed only; readout time was 13.11s vs 3.92s once and supports no speedup claim. The runner count assertion was wrong (192 instead of 480); raw attempt and corrected independent analysis are retained. A counterbalanced two-seed replication then produced exact tuning parity in both new pairs and repeated the 96.87% reduction: 90,978/90,963 on versus 2,848/2,849 off over 480 presentations; independent analysis and provenance checks pass. Across three seeds this remains one narrow orientation-rate protocol, not evidence that settling is unnecessary for task behavior. Learned/task-positive controls, larger frames, and tick termination reasons remain open ([`SUBSTRATE_VISION_SETTLE_K16_ON_OFF_RESULTS.md`](SUBSTRATE_VISION_SETTLE_K16_ON_OFF_RESULTS.md), [`SUBSTRATE_VISION_SETTLE_K16_ON_OFF_REPLICATION_RESULTS.md`](SUBSTRATE_VISION_SETTLE_K16_ON_OFF_REPLICATION_RESULTS.md), [replication raw](../../../evaluation/reports/substrate_vision_settle_k16_on_off_replication_20260925.json), [analysis](../../../evaluation/reports/substrate_vision_settle_k16_on_off_replication_analysis_20260925.json), [lock](../../../evaluation/substrate_capability/vision_settle_k16_on_off_replication_lock_20260925.json)). ([`SUBSTRATE_VISION_SETTLE_ORIENTATION_SCOPE_PILOT_RESULTS.md`](SUBSTRATE_VISION_SETTLE_ORIENTATION_SCOPE_PILOT_RESULTS.md)). A repeated bounded-work scale comparison now measures the global fold versus a Retina-scoped clone at 4/16/64/256 SUnits (three seeds each). All 12 pairs have exact activation/progress parity; at 256 units medians are 3.425s global and 3.243s scoped, while total graph size grows from 1,912 to 30,655 nodes and median global time rises 570× from 4 units. 22/24 arms reach the fixed 1,024 progress cap. A follow-up with opt-in native per-rule attribution exactly matches the uninstrumented states/progress and conserves all candidate/Term counters. It derives 16,384/262,144/4,194,304/67,108,864 extra `NodesOfType` SUnit values traversed/materialized at N=4/16/64/256 for the global fold relative to scoped; this is logical work derived from call counts × known population, not CPU instructions. Aggregate Term invocation counts miss the interior cardinality. A follow-up instrumentation repair records `NodesOfType` interior cardinality in the opt-in native `term_units` counter. The direct global-minus-scoped deltas exactly match the earlier derived values, including 67,108,864 extra node-value work units at N=256; instrumented states/progress match the prior run. This closes aggregate-work observability for this Term but leaves the wall-time gap and natural prevalence open. A 48-arm two-root scale gate then confirmed that changing sheet B shifts sheet A by 0.5 under the global fold at 12/12 seed-size pairs, while a Retina-root-scoped clone gives exact A independence; at 512 total SUnits `NodesOfType` work is 524,800 global vs 512 scoped. The equation oracle, membership and counters pass. This is synthetic rule-level coupling evidence, not natural-workload prevalence or task-level quality ([`SUBSTRATE_VISION_MULTI_SHEET_SCALE_RESULTS.md`](SUBSTRATE_VISION_MULTI_SHEET_SCALE_RESULTS.md), [raw](../../../evaluation/reports/substrate_vision_multi_sheet_scale_20260925.json), [analysis](../../../evaluation/reports/substrate_vision_multi_sheet_scale_analysis_20260925.json), [lock](../../../evaluation/substrate_capability/vision_multi_sheet_scale_lock_20260925.json)). This demonstrates observability and exposes an unsound static read-dependency gap; do not use the declared sets as a sound partition contract until implicit compare-before-write reads are included and remeasured. A source change now declares `SetAttrIfDifferent` keys as reads and writes; the rebuilt native three-worker replay classifies all mapped concrete-resource observations with zero uncovered events (3,232,224 explicit and 153,966 wildcard-covered), while 36 type-index queries remain unclassified by resource and wildcard use still prevents a locality bound ([v1.2 results](SUBSTRATE_HELD_TOPIC_RUNTIME_INTERFACE_TRACE_V1_2_RESULTS_20261001.md), [capture](../../../evaluation/reports/held_topic_runtime_interface_trace_v1_2_20261001/capture.json.gz), [analysis](../../../evaluation/reports/held_topic_runtime_interface_trace_v1_2_20261001/analysis_v1_3.json)). A further finite-effect audit makes `SetAttr`, `AddNodeIfAbsent`, `AddEdge`, and `DelEdge` comparison/probe reads explicit and instrumented. A fresh replay reconciles 3,585,810 explicit and 153,966 wildcard-covered operations with zero uncovered among 3,739,776 concrete-resource observations; 36 type-index queries remain unresolved. Wildcard coverage and other/deferred effects still block a general locality or partition-safety claim. All are `reads_any`-covered, so their dynamic work is measured but their frame boundary is not. Eight ownership/error cases pass (including shared root/subtree/leaf and error atomicity). The PyO3 binding fires one `node_deleted` hook per removed node and none for protected nodes. A pre-locked callback probe found and fixed an early-stop defect: all callbacks and all removed-node events now run even if one callback raises, after which the first error is surfaced. Success, protected-leaf, and raise-then-observer cases pass on the rebuilt extension; reentrancy and callback mutation remain unmeasured ([`SUBSTRATE_TERM_CLEANUP_HOOKS_RESULTS.md`](SUBSTRATE_TERM_CLEANUP_HOOKS_RESULTS.md), [protocol](SUBSTRATE_TERM_CLEANUP_HOOKS_PREREG_20260925.md), [raw](../../../evaluation/reports/substrate_term_cleanup_hooks_20260925.json), [runner](../../../scripts/substrate_term_cleanup_hooks_probe.py), [lock](../../../evaluation/substrate_capability/term_cleanup_hooks_lock_20260925.json)). `evaluation/substrate_capability/thoughtexperiment_predecessor_fanout_cost_breakdown_v1.py::main`, `evaluation/substrate_capability/analyze_thoughtexperiment_predecessor_fanout_cost_breakdown_v1.py::main`, `evaluation/substrate_capability/thoughtexperiment_predecessor_fanout_instrumentation_overhead_v1.py::main`, `evaluation/substrate_capability/analyze_thoughtexperiment_predecessor_fanout_instrumentation_overhead_v1_1.py::main`, `evaluation/substrate_capability/thoughtexperiment_predecessor_fanout_instrumentation_overhead_v1_1.py::main`, `evaluation/substrate_capability/analyze_thoughtexperiment_predecessor_fanout_instrumentation_overhead_v1_2.py::main`, `evaluation/substrate_capability/thoughtexperiment_predecessor_fanout_node_scale_v1.py::main`, `evaluation/substrate_capability/analyze_thoughtexperiment_predecessor_fanout_node_scale_v1_1.py::main` | Python orchestrates serialization, native deep-copy, or guarded Linux process-CoW rehearsal. An experimental public Rust `CowPagedVec<T>` uses a shared radix root and copy-on-write 64-slot leaves. `TypedGraph` stores node/edge records and per-node out/in adjacency lists in CowPagedVec; explicit fork routes support eager copies, shared record slabs with eager adjacency, and shared graph pages. Hash query indexes and most other Substrate state still copy eagerly. Rust executes graph-resident and native rewrite rules. The process-CoW child runs in-place and returns a residual receipt to the primary; no branch state is implicitly merged. A fixed-revision batch API computes the baseline once, suppresses candidate-level primary traces, and checks graph/rule identity after the batch. A Rust mutation journal captures before-images of touched nodes/edges and returns exact branch residuals without a whole-graph diff. Current-source static audit v2 passes all 23 public mutable methods and required hooks ([results](THOUGHTEXPERIMENT_TRACKED_MUTATION_AUDIT_V2_RESULTS_20261004.md), [capture](../../../evaluation/reports/thoughtexperiment_tracked_mutations_v2_20261004.json)); unsafe/interior mutation and concurrency remain unproven. `CollapseModelResult` retains exact per-round forward-look residuals for measurement. Default candidate generation now includes at most four deterministic effect-attribute key swaps per target rule. | **Bounded positive/negative rehearsal demonstrated; Linux process-CoW prototype measured.** Across baseline, +1k, and +10k nodes, process-CoW matches prediction and residual outcomes against serialization and native deep-copy controls; the correct candidate converges and the wrong candidate remains divergent, with primary graph/rule digests unchanged before receipt persistence. Three seeded randomized complete blocks per graph size (seed 20260930) pass all six semantic, control-outcome, and parent-isolation gates. Process-CoW total medians (correct/wrong) are 407/439 ms, 700/621 ms, and 2,930/2,881 ms; same-run native deep-copy medians are 389/394 ms, 587/538 ms, and 2,810/2,764 ms. Process-CoW is about 4% slower than deep copy at +10k nodes and slower at smaller sizes. At +10k, repeated baseline prediction alone costs 1.16–1.19 s and the child dirties 22–107 MB. The runner now records randomized blocks and timing components. The result demonstrates isolated child execution and residual return, but not an end-to-end speed advantage. Follow-up sparse frame-read views and a guarded zero-step readout shortcut preserve exact synthetic outputs; at +10k, the shortcut reduces scoped deep-copy rehearsal by 1.21×/1.27× for positive/negative candidates. The refreshed one-repeat scoped CoW replay remains slower than scoped deep copy (2.22/2.26 s versus 1.96/2.03 s) and dirties 96–105 MiB. See the [frame aperture capture](../../../evaluation/reports/thoughtexperiment_frame_aperture_v1.json), [no-op readout ablation](../../../evaluation/reports/thoughtexperiment_noop_readout_ablation_v1.json), and [CoW v2 capture](../../../evaluation/reports/thoughtexperiment_process_cow_scoped_v2.json). Two fixed-revision process-CoW candidates also pass three randomized complete blocks with deferred receipt writes; cached-baseline batching yields 1.14×/1.03×/1.30× at baseline/+1k/+10k ([batch runner](../../../evaluation/substrate_capability/thoughtexperiment_process_batch_amortization_v1.py), [capture](../../../evaluation/reports/thoughtexperiment_process_batch_amortization_v1.json)). This exhausts the two applicable variants from the current default generator on one synthetic frame; broader candidate diversity and open-ended search remain unmeasured. Earlier serialization/deep-copy comparisons and repaired detached execution are also recorded ([process-CoW runner](../../../evaluation/substrate_capability/thoughtexperiment_process_cow_self_correction_v1.py), [capture](../../../evaluation/reports/thoughtexperiment_process_cow_self_correction_v1.json), [native-copy comparison](../../../evaluation/reports/thoughtexperiment_self_correction_copy_comparison_v1.json), [current 25/50-field census](../../../evaluation/reports/thoughtexperiment_state_inventory_v1.json), [CoW design](THOUGHTEXPERIMENT_COW_SUBSTRATE_DESIGN.md), [measurement plan](SUBSTRATE_CAPABILITY_AND_TRACTABILITY_MEASUREMENT_PLAN.md)). A source-locked fixed-revision batch comparison exactly matches standalone positive/negative outcomes and residuals, with 1.15×/1.25×/1.27× speedups at baseline/+1k/+10k nodes (626/719, 950/1,189, and 4,605/5,863 ms batch/standalone); baseline reuse is measured, but full-state branch copies remain and the 10× target is unmet ([batch runner](../../../evaluation/substrate_capability/thoughtexperiment_candidate_batch_amortization_v1.py), [capture](../../../evaluation/reports/thoughtexperiment_candidate_batch_amortization_v1.json)). A source-locked nonzero-inference journal probe covers added/changed/removed nodes and added/removed edges; three paired repeats plus one warmup match exact conclusions/derived-only residuals and preserve primary state at 0/1k/10k/50k/100k unrelated nodes. At +100k, scoped branch plus journal readout is 9.66 ms versus 3,139.06 ms for whole-graph snapshot/diff (324.9x) on this one-rule probe; fixture construction is excluded. A matched self-correction runner now exposes each nested baseline and candidate residual. At baseline and +10k, two measured repeats plus one warmup pass exact nested conclusions/derived-only parity across serialization, native copy, scoped copy, and scoped legacy-diff backends; positive/negative outcomes and primary graph/rule preservation pass. At +10k, journal scoped rehearsal takes 239.62/215.52 ms (positive/negative): 8.4x/9.0x faster than scoped legacy diff, 5.4x/5.8x faster than serialization, and 3.2x/3.5x faster than full native copy; baseline gains are 1.9x/1.7x. This validates near-10x corrected-rehearsal throughput on one synthetic family and host, while broad task/host coverage and useful-experiment rate remain open. ([runner](../../../evaluation/substrate_capability/thoughtexperiment_delta_journal_inference_v1.py), [capture](../../../evaluation/reports/thoughtexperiment_delta_journal_inference_v1.json), [matched capture](../../../evaluation/reports/thoughtexperiment_branch_delta_journal_v1.json)). The large local readout gain does not establish natural-task experiment throughput; candidate usefulness beyond this one family, broader task/host coverage, useful discoveries per fixed-time budget, and whole-state costs remain open. The default candidate panel now contains six variants (dedup fix, negative guard, and four key swaps). On baseline/+10k, two repeats plus one warmup pass exact nested residual parity against legacy readout for every candidate and preserve primary state. At +10k, summed per-candidate journal medians are 1.12 s versus 12.34 s for legacy readout (about 11x potential candidate throughput, excluding fixture construction); only the designated dedup fix converges (2/2), while the four swaps and guard remain divergent. Candidate breadth increased, but no new useful outcome emerged on this fixture ([runner](../../../evaluation/substrate_capability/thoughtexperiment_candidate_diversity_v1.py), [capture](../../../evaluation/reports/thoughtexperiment_candidate_diversity_v1.json)). A separate path/star/grid run at 10k background nodes passes all six positive/negative nested-residual and isolation gates; journal speedups range from 8.7x to 11.4x ([topology runner](../../../evaluation/substrate_capability/thoughtexperiment_topology_scaling_v1.py), [capture](../../../evaluation/reports/thoughtexperiment_topology_scaling_v1.json)). A fixed-revision six-candidate batch then uses one shared baseline and O(1) graph/rule revision checks; three randomized blocks plus a warmup preserve exact nested outputs and primary isolation at 771/1,771/10,771 nodes. It takes 728/777/772 ms versus 1,156/1,225/1,244 ms standalone (1.50x–1.79x throughput), but only one candidate is useful on this fixture ([runner](../../../evaluation/substrate_capability/thoughtexperiment_fixed_revision_six_candidate_batch_v1.py), [capture](../../../evaluation/reports/thoughtexperiment_fixed_revision_six_candidate_batch_v1.json)). A matched process-CoW batch also preserves exact nested outputs and isolation, but takes 891/877/1,165 ms versus 765/696/909 ms native-copy (0.78x–0.86x throughput); direct in-place predictive looks fail parity, so child branches retain native-copy semantics for internal looks ([runner](../../../evaluation/substrate_capability/thoughtexperiment_fixed_revision_process_cow_batch_v1.py), [capture](../../../evaluation/reports/thoughtexperiment_fixed_revision_process_cow_batch_v1.json)). A branch-creation-only comparison isolates deep copy/scope setup from inference: across eight measured blocks, Linux fork plus matching scope/pipe is slower at baseline/+1k/+10k (3.32/3.25/3.38 ms versus 1.17/2.21/3.03 ms), then faster at +50k (6.83 ms versus 13.06 ms; 1.91x). Both arms expose the same three-edge focus view and preserve primary state. This identifies a large-state setup crossover, but its gain is not present in end-to-end six-candidate batches, which remain slower under process-CoW ([runner](../../../evaluation/substrate_capability/thoughtexperiment_branch_creation_cost_v1.py), [capture](../../../evaluation/reports/thoughtexperiment_branch_creation_cost_v1.json)). At 50,771 nodes, the full six-candidate native batch takes 956 ms versus 1,436 ms standalone (1.50x), while process-CoW takes 1,348 ms versus 941 ms native; exact nested outcomes and isolation pass in both captures ([native](../../../evaluation/reports/thoughtexperiment_fixed_revision_six_candidate_batch_50k_v1.json), [process-CoW](../../../evaluation/reports/thoughtexperiment_fixed_revision_process_cow_batch_50k_v1.json)). A nested phase profile splits model-after prediction and each of its three forward-look imaginations: five measured repeats plus two warmups show bounded looks use 58–64 ms and structural scans 17–20 ms; each nested branch/rule setup takes 12–14 ms while collapse takes about 1.2 ms and readout about 1.4–1.5 ms. Exact batch/standalone outputs and primary isolation pass at 0/1k/10k. This points to repeated internal snapshots as a CoW target, not a demonstrated speedup; evidence remains one-host/one-family ([phase capture](../../../evaluation/reports/thoughtexperiment_fixed_revision_six_candidate_prediction_phase_v1.json.gz)). The isolated paged-vector probe passes value, primary/sibling isolation, and untouched-page sharing gates over 20 size/fanout cells. At 50,771 `u64` slots/fanout 32, one-slot fork/write takes 35.22 μs versus 8.14 ms for `Vec` clone/write; at 771/fanout 32 it is slower (10.36 versus 3.71 μs). Reads and drops cost more, base conversion is excluded, and graph indexes are absent, so this is primitive evidence only ([capture](../../../evaluation/reports/cow_paged_vec_probe_v1.json), [design](THOUGHTEXPERIMENT_COW_SUBSTRATE_DESIGN.md)). A randomized matched eager/shared-slab six-candidate comparison passes exact cross-backend nested outputs and isolation in five measured blocks plus two warmups at 0/1k/10k; paired batch-time ratios are 0.99/1.19/0.94, with substantial block spread and no stable overall gain. Snapshot/setup improves at +1k/+10k, but graph query indexes are still copied ([comparison](../../../evaluation/reports/thoughtexperiment_cow_shared_slab_comparison_v1.json.gz), [runner](../../../evaluation/substrate_capability/thoughtexperiment_fixed_revision_six_candidate_batch_v1.py)). A source-locked release probe on actual path-shaped `TypedGraph` values passes type-index/query and primary-isolation checks at 771/1,771/10,771/50,771 nodes (25 repeats each). At 50,771, eager/shared-slab medians are 8.08/2.44 ms for clone and 7.75/6.15 ms for clone plus one attribute write. This confirms a graph-level slab clone gain while leaving index-copy cost and full-state memory unmeasured ([capture](../../../evaluation/reports/typed_graph_cow_probe_v1.json), [runner](../../../evaluation/substrate_capability/typed_graph_cow_probe_v1.py)). A v2 topology/write-density probe covers path, star, and grid graphs at four sizes and clone/one-write/1%-write modes; all 12 cells pass divergent-sibling, primary-isolation, counts, type-index, and neighbour checks. At 50,771 nodes, shared-slab clone is 1.47–2.42 ms versus eager 6.45–10.65 ms; 1%-write is 2.27–3.42 ms versus 7.65–10.38 ms. This is stronger synthetic graph-level evidence, still without index CoW, whole-state cost, or memory accounting ([v2 capture](../../../evaluation/reports/typed_graph_cow_probe_v2.json), [runner](../../../evaluation/substrate_capability/typed_graph_cow_probe_v2.py)). An allocator-instrumented source-locked follow-up uses seven repeats across the same 12 cells; all drops return logical live bytes to baseline and sibling isolation passes. At 50,771 nodes, eager/shared retained heap is 8.23–10.22/5.84–7.03 MiB by topology; one shared write adds 3,936 bytes and 1% writes add about 1.08 MiB. This excludes allocator metadata, RSS/PSS and the remaining non-graph state ([capture](../../../evaluation/reports/typed_graph_cow_memory_probe_v1.json), [runner](../../../evaluation/substrate_capability/typed_graph_cow_memory_probe_v1.py)). A graph-page comparison then shares adjacency too: all 12 path/star/grid cells pass edge/query parity and sibling isolation. At 50,771 nodes, clone-only graph-page retained heap is 3.26–4.70 MiB, records-only 6.68–7.87 MiB, eager 9.07–11.06 MiB; branch drops restore logical live-byte baselines in all cells ([capture](../../../evaluation/reports/typed_graph_cow_graph_pages_memory_probe_v1.json), [runner](../../../evaluation/substrate_capability/typed_graph_cow_graph_pages_memory_probe_v1.py)). The matched thoughtexperiment batch passes exact cross-backend nested-output parity and primary isolation, but has only three measured blocks plus one warmup: graph-page medians at baseline/+1k/+10k are 879/1,258/981 ms versus 856/878/940 ms records-only and 884/966/1,081 ms eager. This is no stable end-to-end gain on this one family ([compressed capture](../../../evaluation/reports/thoughtexperiment_shared_graph_pages_batch_v1.json.gz)). A higher-repeat six-candidate comparison uses five measured randomized blocks plus two warmups at baseline/+10k/+50k. Exact nested outputs and primary isolation pass across eager, records-only, and graph-page routes. The baseline is tied; graph pages win 5/5 blocks at +10k (866 ms vs 1,009 ms eager/966 ms records-only; 1.27×/1.16× paired median ratios) and +50k (912 ms vs 1,426/2,030 ms; 1.65×/2.15×). This establishes an end-to-end gain on the measured synthetic task family, not natural-task or independent-host generality ([compressed v2 capture](../../../evaluation/reports/thoughtexperiment_shared_graph_pages_batch_v2.json.gz)).  A source-locked fresh-process memory capture records RSS/PSS around full Substrate branch creation at 771/10,771/50,771 nodes: at 50,771, median PSS deltas are 11,308 KiB eager, 4,868 KiB shared records, and 4,448 KiB shared graph pages; smaller sizes tie at this measurement resolution. A separate mixed-mutation graph evaluation passes eager parity, base preservation, nested branch and sibling isolation, and delete/append ID reuse across all three fork routes and sizes. These are one-host synthetic results; the PSS measure includes Python/runtime state, while the mutation probe is graph-level and does not cover every derived index ([PSS capture](../../../evaluation/reports/thoughtexperiment_full_substrate_pss_v1.json), [PSS runner](../../../evaluation/substrate_capability/thoughtexperiment_full_substrate_pss_v1.py), [mutation capture](../../../evaluation/reports/typed_graph_cow_mutation_semantics_v1.json), [mutation runner](../../../evaluation/substrate_capability/typed_graph_cow_mutation_semantics_v1.py)). The v2 mutation evaluation extends parity checks to declared attribute indexes, node-type membership, edge-type buckets, and incoming/outgoing neighbours after mutation, deletion/reuse, nested forking, and divergent sibling writes. All nine backend/size rows pass against eager copies; this covers selected derived maps that are copied eagerly, not randomized mutation order, concurrency, or every index layout ([v2 capture](../../../evaluation/reports/typed_graph_cow_mutation_semantics_v2.json), [runner](../../../evaluation/substrate_capability/typed_graph_cow_mutation_semantics_v2.py)). A v3 follow-up crosses three graph topologies (path, star-augmented, grid-augmented), three orderings of dependent/independent mutations, three graph sizes, and three fork routes. All 81 rows preserve base/sibling/nested isolation and match eager graph and derived-index signatures; this reduces concern that the v2 result depends on one operation schedule or path-shaped graph. The topology families share a path backbone, so independent natural-graph generality is still untested ([v3 capture](../../../evaluation/reports/typed_graph_cow_mutation_semantics_v3.json), [runner](../../../evaluation/substrate_capability/typed_graph_cow_mutation_semantics_v3.py)). A source-locked component profile identified the per-node typed adjacency maps as the dominant remaining graph clone cost. At 50,771 path nodes, eager cloning takes 2.92 ms for `out_by_type` and 2.32 ms for `in_by_type`, while a shared shard-root fork takes 0.05/0.04 μs. A 100k-query mixed hit/miss stream measures 5.2/4.4 ns per lookup versus 3.4/3.7 ns for a flat map; star lookup cost is near parity. A 64-shard persistent map now backs these two indexes in the shared-graph-page route, with eager materialization retained for control backends. The refreshed 81-row mutation-order probe passes primary, nested, sibling, deletion/reuse, and derived-query parity against eager controls across three sizes, three topology families, and three mutation schedules. Fork/write and allocator probes show 39–83 μs graph-page clone medians versus 11.2–15.8 ms eager at 50,771 nodes; clone-only branch retention is 0.39–0.95 MiB versus 9.08–11.07 MiB, and all measured drops reclaim branch bytes. The six-candidate end-to-end batch shows no stable direct-sharing advantage at baseline/+1k, a 1.16× median ratio at +10k (5/5 paired wins), and 1.81× at +50k (5/5); this remains one synthetic family and host, with one useful candidate out of six in every arm. Reusable snapshots preserve the residual contract but do not add consistent speed. CoW is useful as a general isolation and backend-compatibility capability even where a workload has no speedup; a vLLM-style shared-prefix adapter remains future work with separate ownership, reuse, cancellation, and reclamation gates ([index profile](../../../evaluation/reports/typed_graph_cow_index_clone_profile_v1.json), [runner](../../../evaluation/substrate_capability/typed_graph_cow_index_clone_profile_v1.py), [fork/write capture](../../../evaluation/reports/typed_graph_cow_graph_pages_probe_v1.json), [allocator capture](../../../evaluation/reports/typed_graph_cow_graph_pages_memory_probe_v1.json), [semantic capture](../../../evaluation/reports/typed_graph_cow_mutation_semantics_v3.json), [0/+1k/+10k Thoughtexperiment capture](../../../evaluation/reports/thoughtexperiment_cow_sharded_indexes_batch_v1.json.gz), [+50k capture](../../../evaluation/reports/thoughtexperiment_cow_sharded_indexes_50k_batch_v1.json.gz)). A source-locked offline candidate-snapshot study tests GenMint against frozen and equal-exposure/no-consolidation controls. The candidate acquires the intended generator in 25/30 workers and transfers exactly in 400/400 calls across those acquired snapshots; failed cells remain in the denominator, yielding 400/480 overall versus 0/480 for each control. Eager-copy and shared-graph-page candidate evaluations match semantically in all 30 workers. Nonlinear negative tasks produce unadjudicated CapabilityAxis novelty signals but no false acceptance or new DimensionGenerator mint. This is authored-family evidence, not project-held P3-E1 efficacy or general self-improvement. A reusable native snapshot interface now supplies an immutable graph/rule revision to isolated branches. Its source-locked 10k-node lifecycle probe passes sibling, primary, and read-only isolation at fanout 1/4/16/32; six-candidate Thoughtexperiment integration matches exact outcomes and nested residuals against direct graph-page sharing but has no measured speed gain (2 repeats, one synthetic family/host). Most remaining graph indexes and non-graph Substrate fields still copy eagerly; the typed out/in adjacency maps are now shared on the shared-graph-page path. vLLM-style shared-prefix use remains a future adapter target, not an integration ([design](THOUGHTEXPERIMENT_COW_SUBSTRATE_DESIGN.md), [capture](../../../evaluation/reports/thoughtexperiment_snapshot_fork_contract_v1.json), [batch capture](../../../evaluation/reports/thoughtexperiment_reusable_snapshot_batch_v1.json.gz)). See also the source-locked snapshot lifecycle gate ([runner](../../../evaluation/substrate_capability/thoughtexperiment_snapshot_fork_contract_v1.py)), [preregistration](SUBSTRATE_OFFLINE_CANDIDATE_SNAPSHOT_EFFICACY_V1_PREREG_20260930.md), [analysis](../../../evaluation/reports/genmint_candidate_snapshot_efficacy_v1_analysis_20260930.json), [runner](../../../scripts/genmint_candidate_snapshot_efficacy_v1.py)). [write-density/locality protocol](COW_SNAPSHOT_WRITE_DENSITY_LOCALITY_V1_PREREG_20261001.md) and [results](COW_SNAPSHOT_WRITE_DENSITY_LOCALITY_V1_RESULTS_20261001.md), with [raw capture](../../../evaluation/reports/cow_snapshot_write_density_locality_v1.json) and [analysis](../../../evaluation/reports/cow_snapshot_write_density_locality_v1_analysis.json). [five-block +10k/+50k reusable snapshot comparison](THOUGHTEXPERIMENT_REUSABLE_SNAPSHOT_BATCH_V2_RESULTS_20261001.md), [protocol](THOUGHTEXPERIMENT_REUSABLE_SNAPSHOT_BATCH_V2_PREREG_20261001.md), and [independent analysis](../../../evaluation/reports/thoughtexperiment_reusable_snapshot_batch_v2_analysis.json). [productive nonempty residual snapshot result](THOUGHTEXPERIMENT_PRODUCTIVE_RESIDUAL_SNAPSHOT_V1_RESULTS_20261001.md), [protocol](THOUGHTEXPERIMENT_PRODUCTIVE_RESIDUAL_SNAPSHOT_V1_PREREG_20261001.md), and [worker analysis](../../../evaluation/reports/thoughtexperiment_productive_residual_snapshot_v1_analysis.json). A source-locked adoption panel passes 9/9 gates over 15 fresh workers at 0/10k/50k unrelated nodes: full `conclusions` receipts match an independent full-primary delta oracle, while stale revisions and malformed before-images reject without changing primary state. Median apply is 0.380/0.791/1.474 ms; see [adoption results](THOUGHTEXPERIMENT_RESIDUAL_ADOPTION_V1_RESULTS_20261001.md), [protocol](THOUGHTEXPERIMENT_RESIDUAL_ADOPTION_V1_PREREG_20261001.md), and [analysis](../../../evaluation/reports/thoughtexperiment_residual_adoption_v1_analysis.json). `derived_only` may omit injected nodes referenced by derived edges, and the current edge diff omits edge attrs in the original wire format; a follow-up now supports edge attribute deltas and journal before-images with five-worker parity/conflict evidence ([probe](../../../evaluation/substrate_capability/thoughtexperiment_edge_attr_delta_v1.py)). The O(1) revision guard now covers the public mutation surface: semantic stores are crate-private with read-only accessors, and tracked methods cover terms, rules, productions, concepts, and active shape target. The [revision-token audit](THOUGHTEXPERIMENT_REVISION_TOKEN_AUDIT_20261001.md) measured full-fingerprint cost at 3.4 ms for 100 and 179.6 ms for 5,000 entries per store. The new native O(degree) incident-edge reground read matches a full-scan oracle in five workers; at 50k background nodes, local reads take 0.064–0.106 ms versus 708–710 ms for full serialization (synthetic access-path probe, not full reground timing). [results](THOUGHTEXPERIMENT_INCIDENT_EDGE_REGROUND_V1_RESULTS_20261001.md), [protocol](THOUGHTEXPERIMENT_INCIDENT_EDGE_REGROUND_V1_PREREG_20261001.md), [runner](../../../evaluation/substrate_capability/thoughtexperiment_incident_edge_reground_v1.py), [capture](../../../evaluation/reports/thoughtexperiment_incident_edge_reground_v1.json). Full reground scale panel: 50k nodes/128 low-degree deletion proposals cost 5.535–6.193 ms; a 49,999-edge hub deletion costs 267.783 ms (three synthetic workers). [results](THOUGHTEXPERIMENT_FULL_REGROUND_SCALE_V1_RESULTS_20261001.md), [protocol](THOUGHTEXPERIMENT_FULL_REGROUND_SCALE_V1_PREREG_20261001.md), [runner](../../../evaluation/substrate_capability/thoughtexperiment_full_reground_scale_v1.py), [capture](../../../evaluation/reports/thoughtexperiment_full_reground_scale_v1.json). Balanced reground factorial (1k/10k/50k nodes × 1/16/128 local deletions) passes all 45 worker/cell oracle checks; medians stay about 0.07/0.76/5.9–6.1 ms across graph sizes at fixed receipt size. [results](THOUGHTEXPERIMENT_FULL_REGROUND_SCALE_V1_1_RESULTS_20261001.md), [protocol](THOUGHTEXPERIMENT_FULL_REGROUND_SCALE_V1_1_PREREG_20261001.md), [runner](../../../evaluation/substrate_capability/thoughtexperiment_full_reground_scale_v1_1.py), [capture](../../../evaluation/reports/thoughtexperiment_full_reground_scale_v1_1.json). Seeded random-sparse regrounding passes model-derived exact verdict parity at 1k/10k/50k for 50 clean and conflicting deletion proposals per graph; medians are 3.53/4.10/5.57 ms. [results](THOUGHTEXPERIMENT_RANDOM_SPARSE_REGROUND_V1_RESULTS_20261001.md), [protocol](THOUGHTEXPERIMENT_RANDOM_SPARSE_REGROUND_V1_PREREG_20261001.md), [runner](../../../evaluation/substrate_capability/thoughtexperiment_random_sparse_reground_v1.py), [capture](../../../evaluation/reports/thoughtexperiment_random_sparse_reground_v1.json). Randomized mixed-receipt adoption matches an independent full-graph oracle in 50/50 approved trials; all 50 paired stale-edge conflicts reject without proposal writes. Receipts are manual 64-node fixtures. [results](THOUGHTEXPERIMENT_MIXED_RECEIPT_ORACLE_V1_RESULTS_20261001.md), [protocol](THOUGHTEXPERIMENT_MIXED_RECEIPT_ORACLE_V1_PREREG_20261001.md), [runner](../../../evaluation/substrate_capability/thoughtexperiment_mixed_receipt_oracle_v1.py), [capture](../../../evaluation/reports/thoughtexperiment_mixed_receipt_oracle_v1.json). Actual graph-resident rule residuals pass 50/50 complete-graph oracle comparisons through rehearsal, default refusal, and approved commit. One authored rule/locality family only. [results](THOUGHTEXPERIMENT_GENERATED_RESIDUAL_ADOPTION_V1_RESULTS_20261001.md), [protocol](THOUGHTEXPERIMENT_GENERATED_RESIDUAL_ADOPTION_V1_PREREG_20261001.md), [runner](../../../evaluation/substrate_capability/thoughtexperiment_generated_residual_adoption_v1.py), [capture](../../../evaluation/reports/thoughtexperiment_generated_residual_adoption_v1.json). A source-locked concurrent same-revision adoption panel runs 30 races/90 submissions across three fresh workers; exactly one receipt wins and two stale receipts reject in every race, with full-graph winner-only oracle parity. This validates strict stale-writer rejection through concurrent API calls, not parallel native throughput under the Python GIL or merge semantics ([protocol](THOUGHTEXPERIMENT_CONCURRENT_RECEIPT_ADOPTION_V1_PREREG_20261001.md), [results](THOUGHTEXPERIMENT_CONCURRENT_RECEIPT_ADOPTION_V1_RESULTS_20261001.md), [runner](../../../evaluation/substrate_capability/thoughtexperiment_concurrent_receipt_adoption_v1.py), [raw](../../../evaluation/reports/thoughtexperiment_concurrent_receipt_adoption_v1_raw_20261001.json)). A source-locked graph-authored self-correction panel exercises the composer seed end to end: after the adapter activates its stored Rule, both arms generate the gate and rank Program, adopt the same repair, and pass a new-signature continuation oracle. On this task, cost ranking tries one failed low-cost variant before the successful repair; fixed order tries the repair first, so ranking adds work without changing outcome ([protocol](THOUGHTEXPERIMENT_GRAPH_AUTHORED_CORRECTION_RANK_V1_PREREG_20261001.md), [results](THOUGHTEXPERIMENT_GRAPH_AUTHORED_CORRECTION_RANK_V1_RESULTS_20261001.md), [runner](../../../evaluation/substrate_capability/thoughtexperiment_graph_authored_correction_rank_v1.py), [raw](../../../evaluation/reports/thoughtexperiment_graph_authored_correction_rank_v1_raw_20261001.json)). Outcome-conditioned candidate ranking passes 8/8 gates in a three-worker transfer panel: typed outcomes from actually tried candidates alter the graph-authored ordering on fresh instances, reducing median attempts from 2 to 1 while preserving held-out continuation. Evidence transfer is host-managed and this is one authored correction family, not broad learning ([results](THOUGHTEXPERIMENT_CORRECTION_OUTCOME_RANK_TRANSFER_V1_RESULTS_20261001.md), [protocol](THOUGHTEXPERIMENT_CORRECTION_OUTCOME_RANK_TRANSFER_V1_PREREG_20261001.md), [runner](../../../evaluation/substrate_capability/thoughtexperiment_correction_outcome_rank_transfer_v1.py)). A read-only audit enumerates 544 generated variants across 159 active rules, but only `sd_mint_quantum` has the domain-shaped repair candidate; this measures syntax coverage, not useful correction coverage ([surface audit](THOUGHTEXPERIMENT_CORRECTION_CANDIDATE_SURFACE_V1_RESULTS_20261001.md), [protocol](THOUGHTEXPERIMENT_CORRECTION_CANDIDATE_SURFACE_V1_PREREG_20261001.md)). A second independently-oracled fault-injection task removes the recurrence offer idempotence guard: 75 duplicate offers collapse to one on a full-copy repair branch, with zero-progress closure and exact primary preservation in three workers. The repair is source-known, not autonomously found ([results](THOUGHTEXPERIMENT_RECURRENCE_OFFER_IDEMPOTENCE_V1_RESULTS_20261001.md), [protocol](THOUGHTEXPERIMENT_RECURRENCE_OFFER_IDEMPOTENCE_V1_PREREG_20261001.md)). The shared graph-authored `idempotent_mint_guard` outcome class records cross-target success between the two repair tasks (1 versus 0 success traces), but cold structural ranking already picks the correct second-family repair first, so search effort is unchanged at one trial per arm; see [results](THOUGHTEXPERIMENT_CROSS_FAMILY_IDEMPOTENCE_TRANSFER_V1_RESULTS_20261001.md) and [amendment](THOUGHTEXPERIMENT_CROSS_FAMILY_IDEMPOTENCE_TRANSFER_V1_AMENDMENT_20261001.md). A staged cross-family panel shows the shared outcome class correcting a cold-ranking error: episode-wide idempotence offers `[1, 0]` and fails, while transferred history ranks candidate-scoped idempotence first and yields `[1, 1]`, reducing attempts 2→1 in three workers ([results](THOUGHTEXPERIMENT_CROSS_FAMILY_IDEMPOTENCE_TRANSFER_V2_RESULTS_20261001.md), [protocol](THOUGHTEXPERIMENT_CROSS_FAMILY_IDEMPOTENCE_TRANSFER_V2_PREREG_20261001.md), [amendment](THOUGHTEXPERIMENT_CROSS_FAMILY_IDEMPOTENCE_TRANSFER_V2_AMENDMENT_20261001.md)). This remains an authored two-family task with host-managed outcome transfer. A new grid-navigation polarity task provides separate evidence for graph-authored structural composition: the residual contains an explicit `flip` cue, the composer selects a real comparison-flip edit, and the candidate passes four held-out directions in three workers; the fixed candidate generator also puts the flip first, so this is not a search-cost advantage or outcome-ranker transfer ([results](THOUGHTEXPERIMENT_NAVIGATION_POLARITY_REPAIR_V1_1_RESULTS_20261002.md), [protocol](THOUGHTEXPERIMENT_NAVIGATION_POLARITY_REPAIR_V1_PREREG_20261002.md), [amendment](THOUGHTEXPERIMENT_NAVIGATION_POLARITY_REPAIR_V1_1_AMENDMENT_20261002.md), [review amendment](THOUGHTEXPERIMENT_NAVIGATION_POLARITY_REPAIR_V1_1_REVIEW_AMENDMENT_20261002.md), [lock](../../../evaluation/substrate_capability/thoughtexperiment_navigation_polarity_repair_v1_1_lock_20261002.json), [raw](../../../evaluation/reports/thoughtexperiment_navigation_polarity_repair_v1_1_raw_20261002.json), [corrected review](../../../evaluation/reports/thoughtexperiment_navigation_polarity_repair_v1_1_analysis_review_20261002.json)). Selected mechanism-level tests, not end-to-end harness coverage: [imagination](../../../tests/test_imagination.py) and [self-correction cognition](../../../tests/test_self_correct_cognition.py). The automatic-outcome persistence transfer and one-record contamination studies are linked as [persistence results](THOUGHTEXPERIMENT_AUTOMATIC_OUTCOME_PERSISTENCE_TRANSFER_V1_RESULTS_20261001.md) and [contamination results](THOUGHTEXPERIMENT_OUTCOME_CONTAMINATION_V1_RESULTS_20261001.md). The five-arm [outcome-noise dose-response](THOUGHTEXPERIMENT_OUTCOME_NOISE_DOSE_RESPONSE_V1_RESULTS_20261001.md) directly tests one versus two false positives and one false negative against the held-out correction oracle; see [protocol](THOUGHTEXPERIMENT_OUTCOME_NOISE_DOSE_RESPONSE_V1_PREREG_20261001.md). The TypeGraph CoW scaling extension reaches 200k nodes across path/star/grid with exact query and sibling-isolation gates; this does not include full Substrate state ([results](TYPED_GRAPH_COW_LARGE_SCALE_V1_RESULTS_20261001.md), [protocol](TYPED_GRAPH_COW_LARGE_SCALE_V1_PREREG_20261001.md), [raw capture](../../../evaluation/reports/typed_graph_cow_large_scale_v1_raw_20261001.json)). These results do not establish autonomous candidate generation or independent-host generalization. Extend to edge density and connected topology, and retain the instrumentation uncertainty; do not infer natural-task prevalence from unrelated-node insertion. Dynamic cross-frame mutation v2.4.1 passes all nine per-control guards with 102 proof rejections/fallback calls per control and exact checkpoint/final parity; aggregate anchor work remains 2.67x monolithic ([results](SUBSTRATE_CONVERSATION_TASK_SCOPE_DYNAMIC_CROSS_FRAME_V2_4_1_RESULTS_20261004.md), [source lock](../../../evaluation/substrate_capability/conversation_task_scope_degree_selectivity_pilot_v2_4_1_lock_20261004.json), [compressed capture](../../../evaluation/reports/conversation_task_scope_degree_selectivity_pilot_v2_4_1_20261004.json.gz), [per-control audit](../../../evaluation/reports/conversation_task_scope_degree_selectivity_v2_4_1_dynamic_analysis_20261004.json)). Bounded effect-index before/after snapshots are now explicitly opt-in. The [observer cost capture](THOUGHTEXPERIMENT_EVENT_EFFECT_COST_V1_RESULTS_20261005.md) measures 2.51x endpoint-event time and +151,060 KiB RSS/PSS on an authored report-cap stress graph; a [held-out 80-case probe](THOUGHTEXPERIMENT_EFFECT_TRACE_DIAGNOSIS_V1_RESULTS_20261005.md) localizes the changed effect exactly when supplied only the event report and failing node ID. This is interface-level evidence: full rematching and rule-source analysis remain stronger unmeasured baselines, and natural-task diagnosis/repair remains open. See the [cost protocol](THOUGHTEXPERIMENT_EVENT_EFFECT_COST_V1_PREREG_20261005.md), [runner](../../../evaluation/substrate_capability/thoughtexperiment_event_effect_cost_v1.py), [lock](../../../evaluation/substrate_capability/thoughtexperiment_event_effect_cost_v1_lock_20261005.json), [capture](../../../evaluation/reports/thoughtexperiment_event_effect_cost_v1_20261005.json), [analysis](../../../evaluation/reports/thoughtexperiment_event_effect_cost_v1_analysis_correction_20261005.json), [diagnosis protocol](THOUGHTEXPERIMENT_EFFECT_TRACE_DIAGNOSIS_V1_PREREG_20261005.md), [runner](../../../evaluation/substrate_capability/thoughtexperiment_effect_trace_diagnosis_v1.py), [lock](../../../evaluation/substrate_capability/thoughtexperiment_effect_trace_diagnosis_v1_lock_20261005.json), [capture](../../../evaluation/reports/thoughtexperiment_effect_trace_diagnosis_v1_20261005.json), and [analysis](../../../evaluation/reports/thoughtexperiment_effect_trace_diagnosis_v1_analysis_correction_20261005.json). A stronger [full-rematch counterfactual panel](THOUGHTEXPERIMENT_EFFECT_TRACE_COUNTERFACTUAL_V1_RESULTS_20261005.md) now shows one omission branch for either exact rematching or effect trace, with 0.526 ms vs 0.567 ms pooled medians; rematching wins at 32 effects. These authored conjunctions do not establish trace advantage. Production-derived dynamic/deferred rules, lightweight retained-binding attribution, target-blind diagnosis, realistic repair utility, and acquisition costs remain open. See the [counterfactual protocol](THOUGHTEXPERIMENT_EFFECT_TRACE_COUNTERFACTUAL_V1_PREREG_20261005.md), [runner](../../../evaluation/substrate_capability/thoughtexperiment_effect_trace_counterfactual_v1.py), [lock](../../../evaluation/substrate_capability/thoughtexperiment_effect_trace_counterfactual_v1_lock_20261005.json), [capture](../../../evaluation/reports/thoughtexperiment_effect_trace_counterfactual_v1_20261005.json), and [analysis](../../../evaluation/reports/thoughtexperiment_effect_trace_counterfactual_v1_analysis_correction_20261005.json). |
| Exact/approximate inference and optimization | `domains/can_native_orchestration.py::scoped_settle`, `domains/can_native_orchestration.py::multi_start_settle_and_pick_best`, `domains/can_junction_tree.py::junction_tree_exact_settle`, `domains/can_native_bp.py::run_loopy_bp`, `domains/solver_dispatch.py::solve_can_relax`, `domains/solver_dispatch.py::solve_can_exact`, `domains/solver_dispatch.py::solve_can_bp`, `domains/solver_dispatch.py::grow_can_network_from_text`, `domains/solver_dispatch.py::ensure_can_native_engine_loaded`, `domains/solver_dispatch.py::mint_solve_can_exact_goal`, `runners/dsl/src/can_ops.rs::mt_energy` / `mt_frustration_given_state`, `runners/dsl/src/argmin_tie.rs`, `seeds/can_native_reimplementation.json` | Explicit CAN tools over already-built Microtheories. Relaxation updates Rust graph state through loaded graph rules; exact junction-tree/BP use Python-side computation and commit results at their configured boundary. Native energy/frustration primitives score states. The CAN update seed is `self_load_only`; its rules require explicit/dependency loading, not generic boot. Exact solver is opt-in and width-gated; BP is opt-in and noncommitting by default. | **Workload demonstrated; bounded envelope measured for selected CAN cases.** [`test_solver_dispatch_migrated.py`](../../../tests/test_solver_dispatch_migrated.py), [`test_solver_can_adapter.py`](../../../tests/test_solver_can_adapter.py), and [`test_can_native_reimplementation.py`](../../../tests/test_can_native_reimplementation.py) cover selected mechanisms and dispatch. Exact inference results and the junction-tree refusal gate are in [`CAN_JUNCTION_TREE_RESULTS.md`](CAN_JUNCTION_TREE_RESULTS.md) and [`CAN_SOLVER_TRACTABILITY_RESULTS.md`](CAN_SOLVER_TRACTABILITY_RESULTS.md); the loopy-BP comparison records convergence limits and cost against multi-start in [`CAN_LOOPY_BP_RESULTS.md`](CAN_LOOPY_BP_RESULTS.md). The authored native discrete relaxation has an independent local-stability check across 180 draws at N=8/16/32; native candidate/where/binding/effect-attempt counters reconcile against identical uninstrumented runs. A refreshed 66-process sweep covers N=256 sparse/banded and N=128 complete graphs; all runs settle and pass the local fixed-point predicate. Cell medians reach 13.7–23.2 seconds at N=256; RSS is 36.6–46.2 MiB, with substantial run-to-run timing variation. Per-rule/variant attribution localizes 99%+ of sparse N=128 candidate pairs to Hebbian mint/reweight matching; the complete control has 15.8M term invocations in `can_native_note_coactivation`, dominated by `Var`/`Eq`/`Nth`. Paired graph/progress parity and counter conservation pass; per-rule instrumentation median overhead is 2.74× and excluded from workload timing. An exact shared-separator CAN envelope covers 96 generated modular graphs through 35 nodes, varying frame count, local size, boundary dimensions, and domain size. Junction-tree MAP equals conditional frame-wise exact MAP on all cases; measured local assignment counts scale with F·d^(k+m). For fixed local dimensions the conditional algorithm grows roughly linearly with frame count, with exponential sensitivity to boundary and local scope; its traced Python memory was lower than this junction-tree implementation on the tested cases. This is a constructed-cut result, not evidence that learned frames or runtime rule dependencies satisfy the cut model ([`SUBSTRATE_CAN_SEPARATOR_FACTORIZATION_RESULTS.md`](SUBSTRATE_CAN_SEPARATOR_FACTORIZATION_RESULTS.md), [raw report](../../../evaluation/reports/can_separator_factorization_envelope_20260925.json)). A runtime-generated authored-workload replay adds 80 post-native-closure N=8 complete graphs (2-state/3-state × coarse/fine, 20 matched seeds per cell): exact MAP matches independent exhaustive enumeration 80/80, BP is optimal 38/80 and converges 42/80, the baseline rule fixed point is optimal 29/80, and native multi-start is optimal 29/80. The min-fill width is 7, so this direct exact run is diagnostic above the production width-4 gate. It measures runtime-generated states, but not natural task locality or scaling ([`SUBSTRATE_CAN_RUNTIME_GENERATED_INFERENCE_RESULTS.md`](SUBSTRATE_CAN_RUNTIME_GENERATED_INFERENCE_RESULTS.md), [raw report](../../../evaluation/reports/can_runtime_generated_inference_envelope_20260925.json)). An authored Bigville replay adds 100 deterministic resident-specific CAN instances (same five-node topology, authored scenario evidence): exact MAP matches an independent 243-state oracle 100/100; BP converges 35/100 and is optimal 37/100; native 20-start relaxation is terminally stable 100/100 and optimal 16/100. Median costs are 0.111 ms exact, 35.0 ms BP, and 5.19 ms relaxation, excluding its separately recorded 2.82 ms seed loading. This is project simulation data, not natural-world validation ([`SUBSTRATE_BIGVILLE_INFERENCE_ENVELOPE_RESULTS.md`](SUBSTRATE_BIGVILLE_INFERENCE_ENVELOPE_RESULTS.md), [raw report](../../../evaluation/reports/can_bigville_inference_envelope_20260925.json)). Current-source treewidth analyzer cost and a paired exact/BP/native-relaxation quality-cost envelope are measured on 24 synthetic n=5/7/9 CAN graphs. Exact MAP matches exhaustive search on all cases; the width-4 gate attempts 16 and refuses 8. BP converges 20/24 and matches the optimum in 19/24; one converged run is suboptimal. Native 20-start relaxation reaches a terminal fixed point in all cases and matches the optimum in 11/24; its frustration-based selector is scored separately by energy. The measured input network remains unchanged. Timings include extraction, width, triangulation, message passing, exhaustive reference, BP, seed loading, and relaxation. Python tracemalloc excludes native memory. The synthetic envelope does not establish natural workload quality, frame locality, or useful-scale inference ([`SUBSTRATE_CAN_INFERENCE_COST_QUALITY_RESULTS.md`](SUBSTRATE_CAN_INFERENCE_COST_QUALITY_RESULTS.md), [raw report](../../../evaluation/reports/can_inference_cost_quality_envelope_20260925.json)). No frame partition is executed ([`SUBSTRATE_CAN_STRUCTURE_ENVELOPE_RESULTS.md`](SUBSTRATE_CAN_STRUCTURE_ENVELOPE_RESULTS.md), [`SUBSTRATE_CAN_PROCESS_SCALE_RESULTS.md`](SUBSTRATE_CAN_PROCESS_SCALE_RESULTS.md), [`SUBSTRATE_CAN_PER_RULE_ATTRIBUTION_RESULTS.md`](SUBSTRATE_CAN_PER_RULE_ATTRIBUTION_RESULTS.md), [`SUBSTRATE_TREEWIDTH_COST_ENVELOPE_RESULTS.md`](SUBSTRATE_TREEWIDTH_COST_ENVELOPE_RESULTS.md)). A new 21-worker width-one scale check runs exact MAP on runtime-extracted path graphs through 2,048 variables against an independent chain-DP oracle; all answers agree. Median MAP is 173 ms at n=2,048, but min-fill ordering takes 1.17 s, exposing ordering as the current measured bottleneck on this family ([`CAN_BOUNDED_WIDTH_SCALE_RESULTS.md`](CAN_BOUNDED_WIDTH_SCALE_RESULTS.md), [raw report](../../../evaluation/reports/can_bounded_width_scale_20260925.json), [pre-run lock](../../../evaluation/substrate_capability/can_bounded_width_scale_lock_20260925.json)). A paired forest leaf-elimination order preserved exact MAP and width one at 256–2,048 nodes; at n=2,048 it reduced ordering from 1.31 s to 1.18 ms. This is a forest-only algorithm comparison and does not establish safe automatic dispatch or general graph speedup ([`CAN_FOREST_ORDERING_COMPARISON_RESULTS.md`](CAN_FOREST_ORDERING_COMPARISON_RESULTS.md), [raw](../../../evaluation/reports/can_forest_ordering_comparison_20260925.json), [lock](../../../evaluation/substrate_capability/can_forest_ordering_lock_20260925.json)). A benchmark-only guarded dispatcher then classified 24/24 path/tree controls as forests and rejected 42/42 cyclic controls across cycles, ladders, sparse chorded graphs, and cliques; min-fill fallback preserved the width gate and all 49 admitted exact MAP pairs. At n=256 the path/tree ordering-plus-MAP medians fell from about 23 ms to 2.4 ms. This is generated-topology evidence only; production integration and natural workload prevalence remain open ([`CAN_GUARDED_FOREST_DISPATCH_RESULTS.md`](CAN_GUARDED_FOREST_DISPATCH_RESULTS.md), [raw](../../../evaluation/reports/can_guarded_forest_dispatch_20260925.json), [lock](../../../evaluation/substrate_capability/can_guarded_forest_dispatch_lock_20260925.json)). An authored workload audit then found zero forests among 100 Bigville five-node/5-pair models and 80 native-rule-generated complete eight-node CAN models. Exact inference matched the exhaustive oracle on Bigville 100/100; all 80 complete models were width 7 and refused by the width-4 gate. This is counterevidence for applying forest ordering to these two authored workloads, not a natural graph prevalence estimate ([`CAN_AUTHORED_ORDERING_AUDIT_RESULTS.md`](CAN_AUTHORED_ORDERING_AUDIT_RESULTS.md), [raw](../../../evaluation/reports/can_authored_ordering_audit_20260925.json), [lock](../../../evaluation/substrate_capability/can_authored_ordering_audit_lock_20260925.json)). A controlled connected-frame stress run compares full round-robin with a caller event queue on 100 runtime-built Bigville CAN frames under disconnected, ring, and star cross-frame links. All six arms match state at every checkpoint and have independently audited zero full-graph residual; event candidate counts fall 98.3–98.7%. The links are injected stressors and the queue is Python-side ([`SUBSTRATE_BIGVILLE_CONNECTED_FRAME_STRESS_RESULTS.md`](SUBSTRATE_BIGVILLE_CONNECTED_FRAME_STRESS_RESULTS.md), [raw](../../../evaluation/reports/substrate_bigville_connected_frame_stress_20260925.json), [analysis](../../../evaluation/reports/substrate_bigville_connected_frame_stress_analysis_20260925.json), [lock](../../../evaluation/substrate_capability/bigville_connected_frame_stress_lock_20260925.json)).  A 36-worker matched N/E/q replication now records complete separator/bag/child histograms and source-aligned loop-work proxies. Profiles explain why certified lower maximum bag width can still run slower: grid certified-order inner-loop proxies are 14,564/28,644 versus min-fill 12,732/22,284 at q=5/6, matching the observed timing direction. The exact routine is pure Python after native graph extraction; per-call tracemalloc and whole-worker RSS are reported separately ([v1.2 results](CAN_EDGE_MATCHED_INFERENCE_COST_V1_2_RESULTS_20260929.md), [raw](../../../evaluation/reports/can_edge_matched_inference_cost_v1_2_20260929.json), [analysis](../../../evaluation/reports/can_edge_matched_inference_cost_v1_2_20260929_analysis.json)). A three-worker 320-case panel over two model exports and four imposed fan-ins passes exact energy, returned-sign, independent rescore, width, and no-mutation gates. End-to-end separator conditioning is 1.95–1.99× faster than this Python JT baseline over the batch, but fan-in-4 cases are ~3× slower; the result is generated 16-node evidence, not native inference or natural-task prevalence. Report tracemalloc as Python call allocation and RSS as whole-worker high-water; do not attribute it to the native JT allocator. Larger task-derived graph families, optimized baselines, natural topology prevalence, and native factorized execution remain unmeasured. |
| Vision/perception primitives | `runners/dsl/src/vision_primitives.rs::convolve`, `runners/dsl/src/vision_primitives.rs::temporal_diff`, `runners/dsl/src/vision_primitives.rs::foveate`, `runners/dsl/src/deform_metric.rs`, `runners/dsl/src/framebuffer.rs`, `runners/dsl/src/gpu_me.rs`, `runners/dsl/src/ink_topology.rs`, `runners/dsl/src/space_quantize.rs`, `domains/upload_text_read.py::decode_foveated_crop`, `domains/contrastive_verdict.py::contrastive_pick`, `runners/dsl/python/substrate_rs/vision.py::image_from_array`, `runners/dsl/python/substrate_rs/vision.py::convolve`, `runners/dsl/python/substrate_rs/vision.py::apply_pipeline`, `domains/vision_graph.py::RGBEye::build`, `domains/vision_graph.py::RGBEye::_process_frame`, `substrate/world_adapter.py::WorldAdapter::update_vision`, `substrate/world_adapter.py::WorldAdapter::perceive_upload_image` | Mixed native primitives, host adapters and domain-specific ingestion.
