Test links include [`test_vision_change_migrated.py`](../../../tests/test_vision_change_migrated.py), [`test_vision_kernels.py`](../../../tests/test_vision_kernels.py), and [`test_curious_agent_vision_graph_migrated.py`](../../../tests/test_curious_agent_vision_graph_migrated.py); they exercise different paths and do not provide one shared perception score. Historical run 37A's harmful top-two result is independently verified and now reproduced on current source: the 400-pair panel returns 256/400 (64.0%) contrastive versus 322/400 (80.5%) global, with 16 fixed/82 broken and exact paired p=`7.38e-12`; source/data locks and exact global baseline parity pass ([current-source results](SUBSTRATE_OMNIGLOT_CONTRASTIVE_CURRENT_V1_RESULTS_20260928.md), [historical reanalysis](SUBSTRATE_OMNIGLOT_CONTRASTIVE_VERDICT_REANALYSIS_V1_RESULTS_20260928.md)). This measures one host-side classifier on one selected benchmark and is not a general perception or tractability score. The phase-0 `vision_eye` rule path now has a source-locked stable-versus-shuffled learning result: 6/6 fixed-patch cases localize at two positions across three seeds, while 0/6 matched-count shuffled controls do ([results](SUBSTRATE_VISION_EYE_PHASE0_PATTERN_LEARNING_V1_RESULTS_20261004.md), [protocol](SUBSTRATE_VISION_EYE_PHASE0_PATTERN_LEARNING_V1_PREREG_20261004.md), [capture](../../../evaluation/reports/vision_eye_phase0_pattern_learning_v1_20261004.json), [analysis](../../../evaluation/reports/vision_eye_phase0_pattern_learning_v1_analysis_20261004.json)). This is an authored visual mechanism result, not natural-image or general induction evidence. Sequential acquisition now passes across six source-locked workers: with both training orders, stage two retains and localizes both trained positions in every worker; the independent analyzer recomputes RF statistics from weights ([results](SUBSTRATE_VISION_EYE_PHASE0_SEQUENTIAL_TRANSFER_V1_RESULTS_20261004.md), [protocol](SUBSTRATE_VISION_EYE_PHASE0_SEQUENTIAL_TRANSFER_V1_PREREG_20261004.md), [analysis amendment](SUBSTRATE_VISION_EYE_PHASE0_SEQUENTIAL_TRANSFER_V1_ANALYSIS_AMENDMENT_20261004.md), [capture](../../../evaluation/reports/vision_eye_phase0_sequential_location_transfer_v1_20261004.json), [analysis](../../../evaluation/reports/vision_eye_phase0_sequential_location_transfer_v1_analysis_20261004.json)). This is two-location retention in one small rule family, not general capacity. A matched-exposure follow-up found blocked training retained both locations in 3/3 seeds, but one-step interleaving did so in only 1/3; repeated switching also raised input-update work from 75 to 399,975 writes per worker, so temporal interference and transition cost remain entangled ([interleaved results](SUBSTRATE_VISION_EYE_PHASE0_INTERLEAVED_RETENTION_V1_RESULTS_20261004.md), [preregistration](SUBSTRATE_VISION_EYE_PHASE0_INTERLEAVED_RETENTION_V1_PREREG_20261004.md), [capture](../../../evaluation/reports/vision_eye_phase0_interleaved_retention_v1_20261004.json), [analysis](../../../evaluation/reports/vision_eye_phase0_interleaved_retention_v1_analysis_20261004.json)). A five-dose follow-up found 1/3 retention at 1- and 10-step switching, then 3/3 at 100, 1,000, and 4,000 steps; closure stayed near 6.7–7.0 ms/step while input writes varied from 399,975 down to 75 ([dose results](SUBSTRATE_VISION_EYE_PHASE0_SWITCHING_DOSE_V1_RESULTS_20261004.md), [protocol](SUBSTRATE_VISION_EYE_PHASE0_SWITCHING_DOSE_V1_PREREG_20261004.md), [capture](../../../evaluation/reports/vision_eye_phase0_switching_dose_v1_20261004.json), [analysis](../../../evaluation/reports/vision_eye_phase0_switching_dose_v1_analysis_20261004.json)). This suggests a provisional switching-interval boundary, with learning and transition work still coupled. A matched one-step schedule interaction found two-location retention rose from 1/3 seeds at eta 0.0005–0.002 to 3/3 at eta 0.005, with unchanged input switch/write counts across eta; the 100-step baseline also passed 3/3. This supports a plasticity-rate contribution in this small panel ([interaction results](SUBSTRATE_VISION_EYE_PHASE0_ETA_SWITCH_INTERACTION_V1_RESULTS_20261004.md), [preregistration](SUBSTRATE_VISION_EYE_PHASE0_ETA_SWITCH_INTERACTION_V1_PREREG_20261004.md), [capture](../../../evaluation/reports/vision_eye_phase0_eta_switch_interaction_v1_20261004.json), [analysis](../../../evaluation/reports/vision_eye_phase0_eta_switch_interaction_v1_analysis_20261004.json)). A follow-up on three new seeds replicated the direction: baseline eta retained both locations in 2/3 seeds, while eta 0.005 retained both in 3/3, on both the original and a held-out coordinate pair ([held-out results](SUBSTRATE_VISION_EYE_PHASE0_ETA_HELDOUT_POSITIONS_V1_RESULTS_20261004.md), [protocol](SUBSTRATE_VISION_EYE_PHASE0_ETA_HELDOUT_POSITIONS_V1_PREREG_20261004.md), [capture](../../../evaluation/reports/vision_eye_phase0_eta_heldout_positions_v1_20261004.json), [analysis](../../../evaluation/reports/vision_eye_phase0_eta_heldout_positions_v1_analysis_20261004.json)). The coordinate pair has a different separation, and three seeds per cell remain preliminary. A separate geometry-transfer panel localized both targets in all three seeds for the 5×5 solid control and for an interleaved 3×3-square/five-cell-cross pair; post-capture RMS spread diagnostics matched the input masks’ second moments exactly, but did not test shape classification ([results](SUBSTRATE_VISION_EYE_PHASE0_SPATIAL_FEATURE_TRANSFER_V1_RESULTS_20261004.md), [preregistration](SUBSTRATE_VISION_EYE_PHASE0_SPATIAL_FEATURE_TRANSFER_V1_PREREG_20261004.md), [analysis amendment](SUBSTRATE_VISION_EYE_PHASE0_SPATIAL_FEATURE_TRANSFER_V1_ANALYSIS_AMENDMENT_20261005.md), [capture](../../../evaluation/reports/vision_eye_phase0_spatial_feature_transfer_v1_20261004.json), [analysis](../../../evaluation/reports/vision_eye_phase0_spatial_feature_transfer_v1_analysis_20261004.json)). A stricter frozen-response test trained on a square and plus, then presented a never-trained X at both learned locations: localized WUnits won 30/30 held-out X probes and 30/30 seen-shape controls across three seeds; raw-weight dot products reproduce all native activations and winners, and probe weights remain exactly unchanged. This is morphology tolerance at trained locations, not shape classification or new-location transfer ([results](SUBSTRATE_VISION_EYE_PHASE0_HELDOUT_SHAPE_RESPONSE_V1_2_RESULTS_20261005.md), [preregistration/amendments](SUBSTRATE_VISION_EYE_PHASE0_HELDOUT_SHAPE_RESPONSE_V1_2_PREREG_20261005.md), [v1.2 capture](../../../evaluation/reports/vision_eye_phase0_heldout_shape_response_v1_2_20261005.json), [analysis](../../../evaluation/reports/vision_eye_phase0_heldout_shape_response_v1_2_analysis_20261005.json)). A new-seed frozen probe separates these: the unseen X remains correctly localized at trained sites (30/30), while transfer to two untrained sites scores 0/30, with median winning-field distance 5.10 cells and spread 6.57. This supports location-specific fields rather than translation-invariant feature coding for the two-site map ([novel-location results](SUBSTRATE_VISION_EYE_PHASE0_NOVEL_SHAPE_LOCATION_V1_RESULTS_20261005.md), [protocol](SUBSTRATE_VISION_EYE_PHASE0_NOVEL_SHAPE_LOCATION_V1_PREREG_20261005.md), [capture](../../../evaluation/reports/vision_eye_phase0_novel_shape_location_v1_20261005.json), [analysis](../../../evaluation/reports/vision_eye_phase0_novel_shape_location_v1_analysis_20261005.json)). A source-locked four-site expansion does not preserve this capability reliably: only 20/60 trained-site probes and 0/180 held-out-site probes meet exact localization, while the weaker nearest-trained-region score is 50/180. Two seeds cover two training sites each; one covers none. Closure stays near 6.96 ms/step, so this is a representation-quality failure under the tested four-site schedule, not evidence of closure scaling cost ([four-site results](SUBSTRATE_VISION_EYE_PHASE0_FOUR_SITE_MAP_INTERPOLATION_V1_RESULTS_20261005.md), [protocol](SUBSTRATE_VISION_EYE_PHASE0_FOUR_SITE_MAP_INTERPOLATION_V1_PREREG_20261005.md), [capture](../../../evaluation/reports/vision_eye_phase0_four_site_map_interpolation_v1_20261005.json), [analysis](../../../evaluation/reports/vision_eye_phase0_four_site_map_interpolation_v1_analysis_20261005.json)). | Next vary alternation block length with transition-count controls, then test independent locations/features on held-out seeds. A paired site-count × schedule factorial resolves the schedule confound: four-site blocked training localized all four sites in every seed (36/36 probes), while four-site interleaving localized 18/36 probes (per-seed site coverage 0/4, 4/4, 2/4); two-site controls passed under both schedules. Held-out exact localization remained 0/108 for four sites under either schedule, and closure stayed 6.80–7.31 ms/step ([factorial results](SUBSTRATE_VISION_EYE_PHASE0_SITE_COUNT_SCHEDULE_FACTORIAL_V1_RESULTS_20261005.md), [protocol](SUBSTRATE_VISION_EYE_PHASE0_SITE_COUNT_SCHEDULE_FACTORIAL_V1_PREREG_20261005.md), [capture](../../../evaluation/reports/vision_eye_phase0_site_count_schedule_factorial_v1_20261005.json), [analysis](../../../evaluation/reports/vision_eye_phase0_site_count_schedule_factorial_v1_analysis_20261005.json)).  The six-seed replication confirms blocked acquisition in all 6/6 workers (72/72 trained probes); interleaving localizes 39/72 probes and fully covers all sites in 2/6 workers. Both schedules still score 0/216 on held-out localization (nearest-region 69/216 each); median closure is 6.82 ms/step blocked and 6.70 ms interleaved. The schedule effect is reproducible in this authored learner, while spatial interpolation remains absent ([replication results](SUBSTRATE_VISION_EYE_PHASE0_FOUR_SITE_SCHEDULE_REPLICATION_V1_RESULTS_20261005.md), [preregistration](SUBSTRATE_VISION_EYE_PHASE0_FOUR_SITE_SCHEDULE_REPLICATION_V1_PREREG_20261005.md), [capture](../../../evaluation/reports/vision_eye_phase0_four_site_schedule_replication_v1_20261005.json), [analysis](../../../evaluation/reports/vision_eye_phase0_four_site_schedule_replication_v1_analysis_20261005.json)). A six-seed nine-site grid then acquired all nine sites in every worker and selected the correct nearest-site region on 288/288 frozen between-grid probes, while exact held-out coordinate localization stayed 0/288. This is discrete region generalization, not continuous interpolation. The raw runner had a probe-count gate arithmetic error; the corrected independent analysis validates all 450 probes, source lock, activation/winner parity, and frozen weights ([dense-map results](SUBSTRATE_VISION_EYE_PHASE0_DENSE_MAP_INTERPOLATION_V1_RESULTS_20261005.md), [validation amendment](SUBSTRATE_VISION_EYE_PHASE0_DENSE_MAP_INTERPOLATION_V1_1_AMENDMENT_20261005.md), [preregistration](SUBSTRATE_VISION_EYE_PHASE0_DENSE_MAP_INTERPOLATION_V1_PREREG_20261005.md), [capture](../../../evaluation/reports/vision_eye_phase0_dense_map_interpolation_v1_20261005.json), [analysis](../../../evaluation/reports/vision_eye_phase0_dense_map_interpolation_v1_1_analysis_20261005.json)). A source-locked paired square-versus-cross evaluation is now running on six fresh seeds over the same nine-site grid; it separates morphology effects from the already measured square-grid result but has no outcome evidence yet ([preregistration](SUBSTRATE_VISION_EYE_PHASE0_DENSE_MAP_MORPHOLOGY_TRANSFER_V1_PREREG_20261005.md), [runner](../../../evaluation/substrate_capability/vision_eye_phase0_dense_map_morphology_transfer_v1.py), [source lock](../../../evaluation/substrate_capability/vision_eye_phase0_dense_map_morphology_transfer_v1_lock_20261005.json)). The next independent gate is a translated grid origin.
| Shape decode, recognition, transform, dimension inference | `runners/dsl/src/term.rs::evaluate`, `runners/dsl/src/shape_decode.rs::eval_shape_decode` / `eval_transform_decode` / `eval_dim_infer` / `eval_gen_infer` / `eval_recognise_shape` / `eval_meta_shape_mint`, `seeds/shape_decode_loop.json`, `seeds/meta_shape_growth.json`, `domains/reflective_faculty.py::_ensure_shape_decode_loop` | Rust Term evaluators operate over graph-resident signatures/codebooks; graph-authored seed rules invoke selected rungs. Python boot loads the seed and selected host paths construct observations/orchestrate use. Rung activation depends on the loaded seed and codebook; this is not an always-on open-ended learner. | **Mechanism tested and workload-specific results exist.** The feature-gated full-rank 3-D GenInfer path now has exact semantic/refusal evidence across ordered and independently shuffled identities. Its sorted join reduces measured train key-field comparisons 129.5× at N=2,048, and a separate uninstrumented 72-worker capture reduces paired training closure median 29.8% at that size; this is an authored 3-D optimization, not natural-task prevalence ([shuffled work results](GENINFER_FULL_RANK_SORTED_JOIN_SCALE_V2_3_RESULTS_20261006.md), [clean timing results](GENINFER_FULL_RANK_SORTED_JOIN_CLEAN_TIMING_V1_RESULTS_20261006.md)). The “nonlinear” matrix control is an in-frame `x*y` warp; separate `TimeInfer` order-N trajectory extrapolation has measured order-2/order-3 rigid-translation-offset cases ([order-N results](GENERIC_ORDER_N_RESULTS.md)). The source-locked 15-worker TimeInfer changing-map baseline passes order-2 translation and random-refusal controls, but refuses changing linear, changing quadratic, and static quadratic maps without false minting; quadratic-map refusal takes 46–47 ms across four direct fields on these small fixtures ([results](TIMEINFER_CHANGING_MAP_EXTRAPOLATION_V1_1_RESULTS_20261006.md), [protocol](TIMEINFER_CHANGING_MAP_EXTRAPOLATION_V1_1_PREREG_20261006.md), [capture](../../../evaluation/reports/timeinfer_changing_map_extrapolation_v1_1_20261006.json), [runner](../../../evaluation/substrate_capability/timeinfer_changing_map_extrapolation_v1_1.py), [analysis](../../../evaluation/reports/timeinfer_changing_map_extrapolation_v1_1_analysis_20261006.json)). Full-seed GenInfer/mint is now rerun on engine `24073a9be843`: three held-out 2-D generators are recovered, a six-member orbit compresses from six quanta to one generator (declared token proxy 24→12), 400 fixed value-noise nulls mint no generator, and a later unseen member changes from `minted` to `recognized_transformed`; all six gates pass. This is an authored integer-linear workload, not a general learning or natural-task claim ([`SUBSTRATE_GENMINT_MEASUREMENT_RESULTS.md`](SUBSTRATE_GENMINT_MEASUREMENT_RESULTS.md), [raw](../../../evaluation/reports/substrate_genmint_measurement_20260926.json), [analysis](../../../evaluation/reports/substrate_genmint_measurement_analysis_20260926.json)). A separate preregistered direct-call scale sweep on one four-relation shape at B=4/8/12, five calls each, returns exact matrices and candidate counts (6,560/83,520/390,624); median time is 7.398/93.812/437.723 ms at roughly 0.89M candidates/s. This is a local evaluator envelope, not end-to-end closure or general scaling evidence ([`SUBSTRATE_GENINFER_SCALE_RESULTS.md`](SUBSTRATE_GENINFER_SCALE_RESULTS.md), [preregistration](SUBSTRATE_GENINFER_SCALE_PREREG_20260926.md), [raw](../../../evaluation/reports/substrate_geninfer_scale_probe_v1_20260926.json), [analysis](../../../evaluation/reports/substrate_geninfer_scale_probe_v1_analysis_20260926.json)). [A subsequent 24-call bound-scaling sweep (12 scale-by-condition cells) uses one asymmetric L shape at s=1–4 (B=3s): positives recover the exact matrix with zero residual and predicate-corrupted controls refuse; candidate counts follow `(2B+1)^4−1` through 390,624, with positive medians 3.01–452.55 ms (about 1.15–1.25 µs/candidate). Null cases take less time at equal candidate counts, so `checked` is not a complete cost model. This is one direct 2-D kernel family, with no end-to-end or extrapolation claim](SUBSTRATE_GENINFER_BOUND_SCALING_V1_RESULTS_20260926.md), [protocol](SUBSTRATE_GENINFER_BOUND_SCALING_PREREG_20260926.md), and [analysis](../../../evaluation/reports/substrate_geninfer_bound_scaling_v1_analysis_20260926.json). At fixed B=4 and candidate count, a second five-repeat sweep from 4 to 64 predicate-distinguished copies gives 7.676–119.592 ms medians, about 15.58× time for 16× relations on this repeated motif; it does not establish diverse-shape scaling ([`SUBSTRATE_GENINFER_RELATION_COUNT_RESULTS.md`](SUBSTRATE_GENINFER_RELATION_COUNT_RESULTS.md), [preregistration](SUBSTRATE_GENINFER_RELATION_COUNT_PREREG_20260926.md), [raw](../../../evaluation/reports/substrate_geninfer_relation_count_probe_v1_20260926.json), [analysis](../../../evaluation/reports/substrate_geninfer_relation_count_probe_v1_analysis_20260926.json)). A 12-cell pattern-structure extension varies four asymmetric 2-D coordinate sets and shared/paired/unique predicate labels at four relations/B=4. All 60 calls recover the matrix at 6,560 candidates; unique predicates show about 1.55× shared-label median time in this five-repeat probe. Geometry spread is smaller, but this remains one positive transform class ([results](SUBSTRATE_GENINFER_PATTERN_STRUCTURE_RESULTS_20260926.md), [protocol](SUBSTRATE_GENINFER_PATTERN_STRUCTURE_PREREG_20260926.md), [analysis](../../../evaluation/reports/substrate_geninfer_pattern_structure_v1_analysis_20260926.json)). A fixed-signature transform-family follow-up recovers four distinct invertible 2-D matrices in all 20 calls at B=4/6,560 candidates; median latencies are 11.18–11.68 ms with overlapping ranges and no resolved ordering ([results](SUBSTRATE_GENINFER_TRANSFORM_FAMILY_RESULTS_20260926.md), [protocol](SUBSTRATE_GENINFER_TRANSFORM_FAMILY_PREREG_20260926.md), [analysis](../../../evaluation/reports/substrate_geninfer_transform_family_v1_analysis_20260926.json)). [`test_dimension_inference_migrated.py`](../../../tests/test_dimension_inference_migrated.py), [`test_two_novel_orbits_gate_migrated.py`](../../../tests/test_two_novel_orbits_gate_migrated.py), and [`test_meta_shape_arity_migrated.py`](../../../tests/test_meta_shape_arity_migrated.py) cover selected paths. Rung/novelty evidence: [`QUESTION_DRIVER_RUNG2_RESULTS.md`](QUESTION_DRIVER_RUNG2_RESULTS.md), [`QUESTION_DRIVER_RUNG3_RESULTS.md`](QUESTION_DRIVER_RUNG3_RESULTS.md), [`TWO_NOVEL_ORBITS_GATE_RESULTS.md`](TWO_NOVEL_ORBITS_GATE_RESULTS.md), [`SHAPE_DECODE_LIBRARY_SCALING_RESULTS.md`](SHAPE_DECODE_LIBRARY_SCALING_RESULTS.md). A preregistered geometry-distribution extension runs 576 fresh workers over 24 distinct bounded-lattice shapes, eight generator families, matched ablations, and three replicates. All 16 v2.1 integrity/semantic gates pass. Geometry-level exact route rates are 24/24 for each primitive, 21/24 for rotation90→cycle4, and 12/24 for reflection_r→cycle4; residual-bearing and rung1-only routes explain most misses. Cycle4 ablation retains a reflection_r+local-shift route on 6/24 shapes and mints on 18/24. Exact reflection identities are endpoint-invariant and cannot be separated by new geometry. This is a synthetic held-operator distribution, not natural prevalence or induction evidence ([results](SUBSTRATE_DIMINFER_NONLINEAR_GEOMETRY_DISTRIBUTION_V2_RESULTS_20260926.md), [protocol](SUBSTRATE_DIMINFER_NONLINEAR_GEOMETRY_DISTRIBUTION_V2_PREREG_20260926.md), [amendment](SUBSTRATE_DIMINFER_NONLINEAR_GEOMETRY_DISTRIBUTION_V2_1_AMENDMENT_20260926.md), [raw](../../../evaluation/reports/substrate_diminfer_nonlinear_geometry_distribution_v2_20260926.json), [analysis](../../../evaluation/reports/substrate_diminfer_nonlinear_geometry_distribution_v2_1_analysis_20260926.json)). A separate 72-worker full-seed codebook-ablation capture tests rotation, reflections, scale2, cycle4, and three length-2 compositions with GenInfer disabled. Exact intended routes are 9/9 for six families and 6/9 for reflection_r→cycle4; three remaining cases use cycle4 k=2 plus predicate-local residual shifts. Removing requested generators causes one quantum in 51 cases, while 21 remain recognized through alternate paths. Reflection has an exact all-geometry D4 alias (`reflection_r = reflection_c → rotation90(k=2)` and vice versa), so endpoint geometry cannot identify the generating path; separate residual aliases are geometry-dependent. All intact cases mint zero quanta; primitive closure medians are ~0.7 ms and composition medians ~1.6–1.7 ms with startup excluded. This is held-codebook application, not novel nonlinear induction or natural-task evidence; only v1.3 has valid quantum deltas ([results](SUBSTRATE_DIMINFER_NONLINEAR_CODEBOOK_ABLATION_V1_3_RESULTS_20260926.md), [protocol](SUBSTRATE_DIMINFER_NONLINEAR_CODEBOOK_ABLATION_PREREG_20260926.md), [amendments](SUBSTRATE_DIMINFER_NONLINEAR_CODEBOOK_ABLATION_V1_1_AMENDMENT_20260926.md), [raw](../../../evaluation/reports/substrate_diminfer_nonlinear_codebook_ablation_v1_3_20260926.json), [analysis](../../../evaluation/reports/substrate_diminfer_nonlinear_codebook_ablation_v1_3_analysis_20260926.json)). A preregistered full-seed matrix-family reuse study spans six 2-D transforms, three predicate layouts, matched enabled/disabled arms, and K={0,4,16} (324 fresh workers). The corrected analysis finds exact matrix acquisition in 33/54 enabled training cells despite all reporting `recognized_transformed`; all 2,604 rows on 651 applications that select the acquired matrix are predicted exactly. Application routing includes many non-acquired-generator paths. At K=16 family-level paired-cost medians range from −11.44 to +12.14 ms; pooled median is −0.056 ms (27/54 enabled faster), i.e. no stable aggregate break-even through this horizon. The initial analyzer seed mismatch is preserved and corrected analysis disclosed. This expands selected authored linear families only ([results](SUBSTRATE_GENMINT_MATRIX_FAMILY_REUSE_V3_RESULTS_20260926.md), [protocol](SUBSTRATE_GENMINT_MATRIX_FAMILY_REUSE_V3_PREREG_20260926.md), [amendment](SUBSTRATE_GENMINT_MATRIX_FAMILY_REUSE_V3_1_AMENDMENT_20260926.md), [raw](../../../evaluation/reports/substrate_genmint_matrix_family_reuse_v3_20260926.json), [analysis](../../../evaluation/reports/substrate_genmint_matrix_family_reuse_v3_1_analysis_20260926.json)). The live ProblemShape pathway now has a fixed-order induction-economics measurement with per-rule candidate/term attribution and residual/mint/re-decode outcomes ([`SUBSTRATE_PROBLEM_SHAPE_INDUCTION_ECONOMICS_RESULTS.md`](SUBSTRATE_PROBLEM_SHAPE_INDUCTION_ECONOMICS_RESULTS.md), [raw](../../../evaluation/reports/substrate_problem_shape_induction_economics_20260925.json)). A 12-pair randomized all-transition eventization follow-up preserves semantic gates and reduces measured ProblemShape work (263→66 candidate pairs; 2,011→1,008 Term evaluations), while wall-time evidence remains inconclusive (median ratio 0.968; paired-bootstrap 95% interval 0.898–1.132); this is one N=8 recurrent-residual workload, not a general scaling result ([`SUBSTRATE_PROBLEM_SHAPE_ALL_TRANSITIONS_TIMING_V2_RESULTS.md`](SUBSTRATE_PROBLEM_SHAPE_ALL_TRANSITIONS_TIMING_V2_RESULTS.md), [raw](../../../evaluation/reports/substrate_problem_shape_all_transitions_timing_v2_20260925.json), [analysis](../../../evaluation/reports/substrate_problem_shape_all_transitions_timing_v2_analysis_20260925.json)). A larger-population follow-up at N=16/32, six pairs per size, preserves semantic parity and reduces candidate pairs by 81–84% and Term evaluations by 60–66%; timing intervals include parity and N=32 median is 4.3% slower. This is one authored sequence, not an asymptotic or natural-task result ([`SUBSTRATE_PROBLEM_SHAPE_ALL_TRANSITIONS_LARGER_N_RESULTS.md`](SUBSTRATE_PROBLEM_SHAPE_ALL_TRANSITIONS_LARGER_N_RESULTS.md), [raw](../../../evaluation/reports/substrate_problem_shape_all_transitions_larger_n_20260925.json), [analysis](../../../evaluation/reports/substrate_problem_shape_all_transitions_larger_n_analysis_20260925.json)). A separately paired recognition-only workload at N=16/32 also preserves semantic gates and cuts candidate pairs by 82–84% and Term evaluations by 63–67%; wall intervals again include parity (median ratios 1.024 and 0.995). Operation reductions currently have no demonstrated runtime gain across either measured workload ([`SUBSTRATE_PROBLEM_SHAPE_ALL_TRANSITIONS_RECOGNITION_LARGER_N_RESULTS.md`](SUBSTRATE_PROBLEM_SHAPE_ALL_TRANSITIONS_RECOGNITION_LARGER_N_RESULTS.md), [raw](../../../evaluation/reports/substrate_problem_shape_all_transitions_recognition_larger_n_20260925.json), [analysis](../../../evaluation/reports/substrate_problem_shape_all_transitions_recognition_larger_n_analysis_20260925.json)). A detailed N=32 component diagnostic finds candidate host index/event maintenance at ~0.2% of the timed observation path, while native closure accounts for ~98.5%; this is one worker per arm/workload and points to native scheduling/matching attribution as the next profiling need ([`SUBSTRATE_PROBLEM_SHAPE_EVENTIZATION_COST_ATTRIBUTION_RESULTS.md`](SUBSTRATE_PROBLEM_SHAPE_EVENTIZATION_COST_ATTRIBUTION_RESULTS.md), [raw](../../../evaluation/reports/substrate_problem_shape_eventization_cost_attribution_20260925.json), [analysis](../../../evaluation/reports/substrate_problem_shape_eventization_cost_attribution_analysis_20260925.json)). The same full-seed records show subsystem Term reductions translate to only 0.41–0.43% fewer all-rule Term evaluations; rule enumerations are unchanged and index/event graph writes double, which bounds the expected end-to-end timing effect in this workload ([analysis](../../../evaluation/reports/substrate_problem_shape_eventization_cost_attribution_analysis_20260925.json)). Repeated paired all-rule attribution confirms 0.41–0.43% total Term reductions and identifies six other rules whose match candidate pairs rise while their enumeration counts remain unchanged; timing remains inconclusive, and the causal route is unestablished ([`SUBSTRATE_PROBLEM_SHAPE_GLOBAL_RULE_ATTRIBUTION_RESULTS.md`](SUBSTRATE_PROBLEM_SHAPE_GLOBAL_RULE_ATTRIBUTION_RESULTS.md), [raw](../../../evaluation/reports/substrate_problem_shape_global_rule_attribution_20260925.json), [analysis](../../../evaluation/reports/substrate_problem_shape_global_rule_attribution_analysis_20260925.json)). A 12-process population sweep with a frozen-codebook ablation finds triangular (`N(N+1)/2`) `ps_mint` candidate checks on recognition-only streams, with one mint and successful re-decode on recurrent-residual streams; this is rule-specific synthetic economics, not natural residual prevalence ([`SUBSTRATE_PROBLEM_SHAPE_POPULATION_SCALE_RESULTS.md`](SUBSTRATE_PROBLEM_SHAPE_POPULATION_SCALE_RESULTS.md), [raw](../../../evaluation/reports/substrate_problem_shape_population_scale_20260925.json), [analysis](../../../evaluation/reports/substrate_problem_shape_population_scale_analysis_20260925.json)). A 540-worker full-seed reuse extension finds exact four-row prediction on all 1,240 calls using a training-acquired matrix; pooled closure cost crosses at K=16, and the acquisition-conditional median crosses at K=8. The repeatedly missed `shearB × adjacent_01` training layout defers acquisition to its first held-out call (548–595 ms), then reuses the minted generator at about 0.8–1.6 ms per call. This is a selected authored-family economic profile with a material latency-tail failure, not a general tractability claim ([extended results](SUBSTRATE_GENMINT_MATRIX_REUSE_V2_RESULTS_20260926.md), [protocol](SUBSTRATE_GENMINT_MATRIX_REUSE_V2_PREREG_20260926.md), [analysis amendment](SUBSTRATE_GENMINT_MATRIX_REUSE_V2_1_AMENDMENT_20260926.md), [raw](../../../evaluation/reports/substrate_genmint_matrix_reuse_v2_20260926.json), [analysis](../../../evaluation/reports/substrate_genmint_matrix_reuse_v2_1_analysis_20260926.json)). A locked 60-case nonlinear-transfer contrast and fresh-process 60-case replication pass all admission gates after the linear positive control recovers and transfers the exact matrix in 10/10 enabled cases. The quadratic and piecewise transformations induce no operator in 10/10 cases each and fall back to quantum minting; quadratic miss closure reaches 2.042 s in this capture. This directly bounds the tested path to linear induction and exposes a costly nonlinear miss, not general nonlinear-learning impossibility ([results](SUBSTRATE_NONLINEAR_DIMENSION_INDUCTION_V1_1_RESULTS_20260926.md), [protocol](SUBSTRATE_NONLINEAR_DIMENSION_INDUCTION_V1_1_PREREG_20260926.md), [lock](../../../evaluation/substrate_capability/nonlinear_dimension_induction_v1_1_lock_20260926.json), [raw](../../../evaluation/reports/substrate_nonlinear_dimension_induction_v1_1_20260926.json), [analysis](../../../evaluation/reports/substrate_nonlinear_dimension_induction_v1_1_analysis_20260926.json)). The v1 capture is retained as an excluded harness failure ([excluded v1 raw capture](../../../evaluation/reports/substrate_nonlinear_dimension_induction_v1_20260926.json), [analysis](../../../evaluation/reports/substrate_nonlinear_dimension_induction_v1_analysis_20260926.json)). A current-source replication of the full-seed nonlinear-induction gate reuses the exact v1.1 point schedules across 60 fresh cases; all 12 corrected gates pass, and semantic outcomes match the prior process replicate cell by cell. The current source again induces/transfers the linear map in 10/10 cases and induces neither quadratic nor piecewise shear in 10/10 each. The quadratic miss costs 1.07 s median closure (2.10 s maximum), versus 0.47 ms frozen. An earlier v1.2 capture is disclosed as an analyzer false negative after its provenance comparison omitted the locked protocol hash; it is preserved and not pooled ([v1.2.1 results](SUBSTRATE_NONLINEAR_DIMENSION_INDUCTION_V1_2_1_RESULTS_20260929.md), [amendment](SUBSTRATE_NONLINEAR_DIMENSION_INDUCTION_V1_2_1_AMENDMENT_20260929.md), [comparison](../../../evaluation/reports/substrate_nonlinear_dimension_induction_current_source_comparison_v1_20260929.json), [analysis](../../../evaluation/reports/substrate_nonlinear_dimension_induction_v1_2_1_analysis_20260929.json), [raw](../../../evaluation/reports/substrate_nonlinear_dimension_induction_v1_2_1_20260929.json), [lock](../../../evaluation/substrate_capability/nonlinear_dimension_induction_v1_2_1_lock_20260929.json)). A third source-locked coupled-quadratic family, `T(x,y)=(x+xy,y+x²)`, yields no new operator or exact transfer in 10/10 runs while the linear control succeeds 10/10. V1.5 native counters show one GenInfer call per enabled nonlinear stage, 7,812,480 transfer matrix attempts over 20 bases at B=12, and residual rejection of every candidate; training at B=3 takes 48,000 attempts. This localizes the failure to the current linear hypothesis family and shows the search-size cost. The v1.4 EvalCtx counter attempt was inert and is disclosed, not treated as evidence ([v1.5 results](SUBSTRATE_NONLINEAR_DIMENSION_INDUCTION_V1_5_RESULTS_20261005.md), [protocol](SUBSTRATE_NONLINEAR_DIMENSION_INDUCTION_V1_5_PREREG_20261005.md), [analysis](../../../evaluation/reports/substrate_nonlinear_dimension_induction_v1_5_analysis_20261005.json), [capture](../../../evaluation/reports/substrate_nonlinear_dimension_induction_v1_5_20261005.json), [v1.4 amendment](SUBSTRATE_NONLINEAR_DIMENSION_INDUCTION_V1_4_AMENDMENT_20261005.md)). A bounded quadratic learner design is now specified but not implemented; exact preflight shows the old training geometry is rank 4, while a revised fixture uniquely identifies the five quadratic coefficients and transfers to six held-outs ([design](SUBSTRATE_NONLINEAR_DIMENSION_INDUCTION_V2_DESIGN_20261006.md), [preflight](../../../evaluation/reports/nonlinear_quadratic_basis_preflight_v1_20261006.json), [runner](../../../evaluation/substrate_capability/nonlinear_quadratic_basis_preflight_v1.py)). A cross-family inventory now distinguishes bounded GenInfer matrix acquisition from order-N TimeInfer, symbolic MetaShapeMint, gated DepthInfer/BlinkBridge, direct unintegrated SupportInfer/RatioInduce, and grammar-bounded executable synthesis; these mechanisms have distinct evidence and do not support one aggregate learning claim ([induction-surface inventory](SUBSTRATE_INDUCTION_SURFACE_INVENTORY_20260926.md)). The companion census across 826 seed manifests finds six `TimeInfer` manifests and one each for `GenInfer`, `DepthInfer`, `BlinkBridge`, and `MetaShapeMint`, with no literal seed invocation for `RatioInduce`, `SupportInfer`, or `SynthEnumerate`; this does not prove runtime loading or reachability ([source census](../../../evaluation/reports/substrate_induction_seed_census_v1_20260926.json), [runner](../../../scripts/substrate_induction_seed_census_v1.py)).  A current-source locked held-out panel tests 12 asymmetric polyomino orbits across six transforms. Task-adapter canonical-key returns are 71/72 clean, 284/288 at sigma 8, and 273/288 at sigma 12. A source-locked graph replay covers all 756 saved observations and 85 unique perceived-signature pairs: positive cases yield 636 `recognized_transformed` and 12 exact `recognized`; all 108 added-cell controls yield `minted` with no transform edge. The repeated H06_C/180-degree case is a valid `cycle4` alias the adapter cannot label; 12 sigma-12 positive misses are exact identity after perception. A direct clean-control replay confirms minting creates a `CapabilityAxis`. These results separate adapter compatibility, graph classification, transform aliasing, perceptual collapse, and novelty acquisition; they do not establish general correctness, false-mint prevalence, runner](../../../evaluation/substrate_capability/induce_concern_key_census_v1.py)). A shared-graph test of five exact repeats and ten distinct-shape pairs finds `minted`→`recognized_duplicate` in all 15 sequences; the five targets have distinct D4 orbits but share the same seven-`cell` predicate key, directly exposing key-based suppression of distinct structures under this shape-family path. It does not establish whether broad predicate identity is wrong for every caller. The D4 `name_hint` comparison preserves exact-repeat deduplication (5/5) and separates distinct shapes (10/10) in the frozen panel, confirming the key drives the difference there; the AST caller census finds 204 direct Python calls, including 16 explicit-key domain calls with heterogeneous intent. A source-locked 100-episode, 20-way Omniglot evaluation also exercises three existing graph-Term recognizers on real online strokes: point-set distance scores 66.8% (95% alphabet-cluster interval 62.55–71.10%), ordered DTW 52.75%, and deformable fit 54.25%, versus 5% chance. This is application of held structure through a host adapter, not concept learning or broad transfer ([v1.2 results](SUBSTRATE_OMNIGLOT_ONE_SHOT_CURRENT_V1_2_RESULTS_20260929.md), [analysis](../../../evaluation/reports/omniglot_one_shot_current_v1_2_20260929_analysis.json), [capture](../../../evaluation/reports/omniglot_one_shot_current_v1_2_20260929_raw.jsonl)). A follow-up 2,700-cell Omniglot scale panel varies 5/10/20 nested candidates and 16/32/64-point trajectories across 100 matched episodes. All six gates pass. For point-set distance, 20-way accuracy is 60.5–66.8% across trajectory lengths (5% chance); at 20-way/64 points median worker time is 16.27 ms, with a source-derived 400 candidate-distance evaluations per episode. This finite Term/adapter envelope is not a native operation count or asymptotic bound ([v1.2 scale results](SUBSTRATE_OMNIGLOT_TERM_COST_SCALE_V1_2_RESULTS_20260929.md), [analysis](../../../evaluation/reports/omniglot_term_cost_scale_v1_2_20260929_analysis.json), [capture](../../../evaluation/reports/omniglot_term_cost_scale_v1_2_20260929_raw.jsonl)). A further source-locked transfer panel uses 100 one-shot episodes from the disjoint Omniglot background alphabet split (26 eligible alphabets). It passes all seven gates and scores 67.0% with PointSetDist (95% alphabet-cluster interval 57.0–75.4%; chance 5%). The prior evaluation-split result is 66.8%, but the exploratory unpaired split-difference interval is wide (−10.81 to +9.50 points), so equivalence is not established ([background results](SUBSTRATE_OMNIGLOT_ONE_SHOT_BACKGROUND_V1_RESULTS_20260929.md), [analysis](../../../evaluation/reports/omniglot_one_shot_background_v1_20260929_analysis.json), [capture](../../../evaluation/reports/omniglot_one_shot_background_v1_20260929_raw.jsonl)). A separate source-locked QuickDraw one-shot panel tests eight fixed single-stroke classes with 100 episodes and 4,000 shared queries per graph-Term method. PointSetDist scores 45.7% (95% class-cluster interval 33.9–57.9%; 12.5% chance), ordered DTW 37.1%, and DeformDist 35.9%; all six integrity gates pass. This extends application evidence beyond Omniglot but has no writer IDs, fixed classes, and only eight uncertainty clusters ([results](SUBSTRATE_QUICKDRAW_ONE_SHOT_V1_RESULTS_20260929.md), [protocol](SUBSTRATE_QUICKDRAW_ONE_SHOT_V1_PREREG_20260929.md), [analysis](../../../evaluation/reports/quickdraw_one_shot_v1_20260929_analysis.json), [capture](../../../evaluation/reports/quickdraw_one_shot_v1_20260929_raw.jsonl), [lock](../../../evaluation/substrate_capability/quickdraw_one_shot_v1_20260929_lock.json)). A direct native SynthEnumerate comparison on one coupled quadratic map finds training-consistent `Plus, Times, TupleOf` programs in 10/10 runs but exact held-out transfer in 0/10; the `Plus, TupleOf` ablation finds none and the linear control transfers in 10/10. This locates a bounded overfit/disambiguation gap, not primitive minting or broad synthesis ability ([results](SUBSTRATE_SYNTHENUMERATE_COUPLED_QUADRATIC_V1_3_RESULTS_20261005.md), [protocol](SUBSTRATE_SYNTHENUMERATE_COUPLED_QUADRATIC_V1_PREREG_20261005.md), [runner](../../../scripts/substrate_synthenumerate_coupled_quadratic_v1_3.py), [analyzer](../../../scripts/analyze_substrate_synthenumerate_coupled_quadratic_v1_3.py), [lock](../../../evaluation/substrate_capability/synthenumerate_coupled_quadratic_v1_3_20261005_lock.json), [capture](../../../evaluation/reports/synthenumerate_coupled_quadratic_v1_3_20261005_raw.jsonl), [analysis](../../../evaluation/reports/synthenumerate_coupled_quadratic_v1_3_20261005_analysis.json)). The parsed-codebook cache panel passes all 12 checks across 80 workers, reducing visits by 96.97–99.31% and improving median closure by 4.32–8.71× on one authored singleton-name route ([results](SHAPEDECODE_NAME_APERTURE_CACHE_V1_RESULTS_20261005.md), [protocol](SHAPEDECODE_NAME_APERTURE_CACHE_V1_PREREG_20261005.md), [capture](../../../evaluation/reports/shapedecode_name_aperture_cache_v1_20261005.json), [analysis](../../../evaluation/reports/shapedecode_name_aperture_cache_v1_analysis_20261005.json)). The 14-worker semantic panel passes 12 checks for case variation, duplicate names, None/empty scopes, and reversed aperture ordering ([results](SHAPEDECODE_NAME_APERTURE_SEMANTICS_V1_RESULTS_20261005.md), [protocol](SHAPEDECODE_NAME_APERTURE_SEMANTICS_V1_PREREG_20261005.md), [capture](../../../evaluation/reports/shapedecode_name_aperture_semantics_v1_20261005.json), [analysis](../../../evaluation/reports/shapedecode_name_aperture_semantics_v1_analysis_20261005.json)). Surface(Text), malformed records, exclusions, mutation lifecycle, production-derived workloads, and natural-task prevalence remain open. A source-hashed census finds 44 scoped ShapeDecode Terms across 11 seed files: 3 under MaxOver, 3 under Argmax, 16 in Filter predicates, and 22 in other contexts ([census results](SHAPEDECODE_SEED_APERTURE_REUSE_V1_RESULTS_20261005.md), [protocol](SHAPEDECODE_SEED_APERTURE_REUSE_V1_PROTOCOL_20261005.md), [capture](../../../evaluation/reports/shapedecode_seed_aperture_reuse_v1_20261005.json), [analysis](../../../evaluation/reports/shapedecode_seed_aperture_reuse_v1_analysis_20261005.json)). This is source inventory only; activation and runtime frequency remain unknown. The current-source verdict-comparator replay independently passes all 11 checks over 80 fresh workers and reproduces the cache reduction from 2P² to 2P visits with 2.83–11.81× median speedups across 16–128 VdSignature parts ([results](SHAPEDECODE_VERDICT_COMPARATOR_CACHE_V1_RESULTS_20261005.md), [protocol](SHAPEDECODE_VERDICT_COMPARATOR_CACHE_V1_PREREG_20261005.md), [capture](../../../evaluation/reports/shapedecode_verdict_comparator_cache_v1_20261005.json), [analysis](../../../evaluation/reports/shapedecode_verdict_comparator_cache_v1_analysis_20261005.json)). This is one authored seed arm, not ordinary-operation prevalence. Applicability audit: the active shape_decode_loop contains three global and zero name-scoped ShapeDecode Terms; the current cache branch therefore does not affect its observed three-call live helper route ([scope-audit runner](../../../evaluation/substrate_capability/audit_shape_decode_seed_scope_v1.py), [report](../../../evaluation/reports/shape_decode_seed_scope_audit_v1_20261005.json), [live helper analysis](../../../evaluation/reports/live_helper_work_capture_v1_4_analysis_20261005.json)). A source-locked phase-0 spatial-learning replication transfers square-over-cross held-out nearest-region performance to a shifted site grid and larger 5×5 span: 288/288 versus 210/288 across six paired seeds; both retain zero exact held-out coordinate localization. The manipulation also changes active-cell count (25 versus 9), and remains an authored map task ([v2.1 results](SUBSTRATE_VISION_EYE_PHASE0_DENSE_MAP_MORPHOLOGY_TRANSFER_V2_1_RESULTS_20261005.md), [capture](../../../evaluation/reports/vision_eye_phase0_dense_map_morphology_transfer_v2_1_20261005.json), [analysis](../../../evaluation/reports/vision_eye_phase0_dense_map_morphology_transfer_v2_1_analysis_20261005.json)). A post-hoc pixel-centroid control recovers exact stimulus coordinates and scores all held-out regions in both panels, demonstrating that absolute position is directly encoded in the input; this is not evidence of position-invariant visual learning ([baseline analysis](SUBSTRATE_VISION_EYE_COORDINATE_BASELINES_POSTHOC_V1_RESULTS_20261005.md), [report](../../../evaluation/reports/vision_eye_phase0_coordinate_baselines_posthoc_v1_20261005.json)). A deterministic post-hoc frozen-weight replay applies 25/50/75% active-pixel dropout and 25/50% background additions to the v2.1 held-out masks. The learned weight-sum readout often resists background addition better than the centroid, while centroid remains stronger under dropout; this is analytical inference replay, not graph scheduler execution ([results](SUBSTRATE_VISION_EYE_CORRUPTION_REPLAY_POSTHOC_V1_2_RESULTS_20261005.md), [report](../../../evaluation/reports/vision_eye_corruption_replay_posthoc_v1_2_20261005.json)). Cache performance results apply to the name-scoped authored comparator routes and are not current live-ladder speedups. A paired full-seed 64/512-shard GenInfer closure gate passes exact semantic/work parity on 15 cases and 30 fresh workers; the shard configurations show no reliable closure-cost difference. Positive training medians range from 54 to 861 ms at 64 shards and 52 to 872 ms at 512 shards as relation count grows from 4 to 64. This is a bounded synthetic cost result; natural-task prevalence and the live whole-Substrate route remain open ([results](SUBSTRATE_GENINFER_COW_SHARD_CLOSURE_V1_RESULTS_20261006.md), [protocol](SUBSTRATE_GENINFER_COW_SHARD_CLOSURE_V1_PREREG_20261006.md), [runner](../../../evaluation/substrate_capability/substrate_geninfer_cow_shard_closure_v1.py), [analyzer](../../../evaluation/substrate_capability/analyze_substrate_geninfer_cow_shard_closure_v1.py), [analysis](../../../evaluation/reports/substrate_geninfer_cow_shard_closure_v1_analysis_20261006.json)). A source-locked re-analysis of six executions across two repeated live-runtime self-report prompts observes `DimInfer`/`ShapeDecode` only on one route and no `GenInfer` terms; the repeated prompts do not estimate natural-task prevalence ([pilot results](LIVE_RUNTIME_DIM_INFER_FREQUENCY_V1_RESULTS_20261006.md), [protocol](LIVE_RUNTIME_DIM_INFER_FREQUENCY_V1_PREREG_20261006.md), [capture](../../../evaluation/reports/live_runtime_dim_infer_frequency_v1_20261006.json), [runner](../../../evaluation/substrate_capability/live_runtime_dim_infer_frequency_v1.py), [lock](../../../evaluation/substrate_capability/live_runtime_dim_infer_frequency_v1_lock_20261006.json)). A task-diverse five-condition, ten-worker live route replay finds `DimInfer`/`ShapeDecode` on every condition and no generic `GenInfer`; it remains an authored, selected panel, not natural prevalence ([results](LIVE_CONVERSATION_TASK_INFERENCE_COUNTERS_V1_RESULTS_20261006.md), [protocol](LIVE_CONVERSATION_TASK_INFERENCE_COUNTERS_V1_PREREG_20261006.md), [capture](../../../evaluation/reports/live_conversation_task_inference_counters_v1_20261006.json), [runner](../../../evaluation/substrate_capability/live_conversation_task_inference_counters_v1.py), [lock](../../../evaluation/substrate_capability/live_conversation_task_inference_counters_v1_lock_20261006.json)). A full-counter-map recapture of the same ten-worker panel confirms zero `gen_infer_requests`, kernels, matrix attempts, and `MetaShapeMint` evaluations in those selected routes; natural traffic prevalence remains unknown ([v1.1 results](LIVE_CONVERSATION_TASK_INFERENCE_COUNTERS_V1_1_RESULTS_20261006.md), [protocol](LIVE_CONVERSATION_TASK_INFERENCE_COUNTERS_V1_1_PREREG_20261006.md), [capture](../../../evaluation/reports/live_conversation_task_inference_counters_v1_1_20261006.json), [runner](../../../evaluation/substrate_capability/live_conversation_task_inference_counters_v1_1.py), [lock](../../../evaluation/substrate_capability/live_conversation_task_inference_counters_v1_1_lock_20261006.json)). | Trace all 16 domain call sites through observation construction and returned-key use. Native `SynthEnumerate` performs bottom-up enumeration under a supplied operator grammar, examples, and explicit budget/candidate bounds; native `ProgEval` interprets supported Program IR under fuel. The graph-authored seed manages rule-level proposal/evaluation/status transitions. Python adapters ingest specifications/examples, orchestrate selected workloads, and record results. The optional Rust source reader converts a supported syntax subset to Program IR; it is not the learner. Code-writing and write-codebook growth are adjacent acquisition routes, not measured enumerative-synthesis gains. | **Workload results and counterevidence exist.** [`test_synth_enumerate_migrated.py`](../../../tests/test_synth_enumerate_migrated.py), [`test_program_synthesis_migrated.py`](../../../tests/test_program_synthesis_migrated.py), [`test_prog_synthesis_battery_migrated.py`](../../../tests/test_prog_synthesis_battery_migrated.py), and [`test_program_synthesis_grounding_migrated.py`](../../../tests/test_program_synthesis_grounding_migrated.py) cover selected mechanisms/workloads. [`DESIGN_REVIEW_CAPABILITY_EXPANSION_PREREG.md`](DESIGN_REVIEW_CAPABILITY_EXPANSION_PREREG.md) records a specific Occam-pricing/generalisation concern; task-family evidence includes [`PBE_STRING_BENCHMARK_RESULTS.md`](../program_synthesis/PBE_STRING_BENCHMARK_RESULTS.md), [`PROGRAM_SYNTHESIS_RUNG3_SORT_PRICING_RESULTS.md`](../program_synthesis/PROGRAM_SYNTHESIS_RUNG3_SORT_PRICING_RESULTS.md), [`PROGRAM_SYNTHESIS_STRUCTURAL_CEILING_RUNG1_RESULTS.md`](../program_synthesis/PROGRAM_SYNTHESIS_STRUCTURAL_CEILING_RUNG1_RESULTS.md), and [`PROGRAM_SYNTHESIS_STRUCTURAL_CEILING_RUNG2_RUNG3_RESULTS.md`](../program_synthesis/PROGRAM_SYNTHESIS_STRUCTURAL_CEILING_RUNG2_RUNG3_RESULTS.md). A current-checkout direct PBE rerun reproduces 12/26 typed-default, 17/26 fixed-grammar, and 20/26 after one high-budget escalation; its per-task artifact and limits are in [`SUBSTRATE_PROGRAM_SYNTHESIS_PBE_RECHECK_RESULTS.md`](SUBSTRATE_PROGRAM_SYNTHESIS_PBE_RECHECK_RESULTS.md). A fresh preregistered 22-task integer-list direct-search pilot passes 14/22 typed-default and 16/22 fixed grammar at 200,000 candidates/task; there are two held-out false accepts per arm and six shared failures. This narrows the bounded enumerator profile on one fresh synthetic family, but still does not exercise residual routing, rung-three induction, mint/adoption, or transfer ([`SUBSTRATE_LIST_PBE_PILOT_RESULTS.md`](SUBSTRATE_LIST_PBE_PILOT_RESULTS.md), [raw report](../../../evaluation/reports/substrate_list_pbe_pilot_20260925.json), [pre-run lock](../../../evaluation/substrate_capability/list_pbe_pilot_lock_20260925.json)). A discovery-only run of the full P4 path on that now-known suite exactly reproduces 22 outcomes after a read-back instrumentation correction: 13/22 held-out passes, 3 accepted holdout failures, 6 gaps; 13 acceptances use exemplar retrieval and 9 cases reach enumeration. This characterizes one synthetic route envelope, not blind generalization or natural-task prevalence ([`SUBSTRATE_LIST_PBE_PIPELINE_PILOT_RESULTS.md`](SUBSTRATE_LIST_PBE_PIPELINE_PILOT_RESULTS.md), [corrected raw report](../../../evaluation/reports/substrate_list_pbe_pipeline_instrumented_replication_20260925.json), [initial raw report](../../../evaluation/reports/substrate_list_pbe_pipeline_pilot_20260925.json)). A new pre-locked 30-task composition-family pilot runs the full P4 path against the frozen starter-codebook/no-bigram ablation and repeats main. Both conditions pass 14/30, accept two additional holdout failures, and gap on 14; all paired task outputs/IR/read-backs match exactly, and the main replay fingerprint matches. 13/17 enumerator calls hit the candidate ceiling. The ablation retains the exemplar store, so the zero difference is scoped to the removed added constructions/bigram layer and this generated task sample ([`SUBSTRATE_LIST_PBE_COMPOSITION_BENCHMARK_RESULTS.md`](SUBSTRATE_LIST_PBE_COMPOSITION_BENCHMARK_RESULTS.md), [raw report](../../../evaluation/reports/substrate_list_pbe_composition_benchmark_amended_20260925.json), [list output analysis](../../../evaluation/reports/substrate_list_pbe_composition_analysis_20260925.json), [amended lock](../../../evaluation/substrate_capability/list_pbe_composition_benchmark_amended_lock_20260925.json)). A pre-locked 24-task string transfer pilot crosses to string-valued inputs/outputs and six strata. Main and frozen arms both pass 12/24 with two accepted false positives and ten gaps; all 12 passes come through retrieved exemplars, while all eight enumerator entries reach the 200k cap without acceptance. Main replay matches exactly. This identifies template retrieval as the only successful route on this pilot and shows replacement as a consistent search gap; it is still a generated, supported-operator suite ([`SUBSTRATE_STRING_PBE_TRANSFER_PILOT_RESULTS.md`](SUBSTRATE_STRING_PBE_TRANSFER_PILOT_RESULTS.md), [raw report](../../../evaluation/reports/substrate_string_pbe_transfer_pilot_20260926.json), [pre-run lock](../../../evaluation/substrate_capability/string_pbe_transfer_lock_20260926.json), [independent fixture audit](../../../evaluation/reports/substrate_string_pbe_transfer_analysis_20260926.json)). A preregistered 18-task contrast run then bypassed retrieval and added a 500k-candidate escalation arm: all arms scored 5/18 held-out passes, six accepted holdout failures, and seven gaps; main replay matched exactly. Enumeration recovered some supported transformations without retrieval, while escalation added search cost without outcome gains. The generated suite and its false-acceptance profile do not establish general induction ([`SUBSTRATE_STRING_PBE_CONTRAST_RESULTS.md`](SUBSTRATE_STRING_PBE_CONTRAST_RESULTS.md), [raw](../../../evaluation/reports/substrate_string_pbe_contrast_20260925.json), [lock](../../../evaluation/substrate_capability/string_pbe_contrast_lock_20260925.json)). The direct-PBE acquisition/application pilot measures three selected string tasks using native `evaluate` on the exact returned Term (not reflected Program IR / `ProgEval`): median search 0.08–101.60 ms for 59–198,949 candidates, versus ~2.6–5.0 µs per repeated native-evaluator call after warmup. For two larger tasks, measured two/five repeated acquire-and-use operations cost 140/370 ms and 188/494 ms, while one search plus 2/5 applications cost ~72.8/101.6 ms; this is a selected repeated-identical-spec microbenchmark, not a natural reuse estimate or end-to-end cost ([`SUBSTRATE_SYNTHESIS_ACQUISITION_APPLICATION_RESULTS.md`](SUBSTRATE_SYNTHESIS_ACQUISITION_APPLICATION_RESULTS.md), [preregistration](SUBSTRATE_SYNTHESIS_ACQUISITION_APPLICATION_PREREG_20260925.md), [raw](../../../evaluation/reports/substrate_synthesis_acquisition_application_20260925.json), [lock](../../../evaluation/substrate_capability/synthesis_acquisition_application_lock_20260925.json)). A full P4/ProgEval retention probe on one known reverse-string success measures boot, route, seven-row verification, IR export/restore, and repeated application separately. The route accepted by `retrieved_exemplar`, so it is not new induction evidence. The 161-byte IR added five graph nodes; root-only `remove_node` left four, with +4/+40/+400 retained Term nodes and +316/+3,160/+31,600 compact whole-graph JSON bytes after 1/10/100 add/remove cycles. Current RSS deltas (0–304 KiB) were inconclusive; shared-child behavior and workload impact remain unmeasured. Existing helper patterns that remove only the root may accumulate unreferenced term children ([`SUBSTRATE_P4_PROGRAM_IR_REUSE_RESULTS.md`](SUBSTRATE_P4_PROGRAM_IR_REUSE_RESULTS.md), [preregistration](SUBSTRATE_P4_PROGRAM_IR_REUSE_PREREG_20260925.md), [raw](../../../evaluation/reports/substrate_p4_program_ir_reuse_20260925.json), [lock](../../../evaluation/substrate_capability/p4_program_ir_reuse_lock_20260925.json)). A source/native-locked 40-cell bounded nonlinear-map acquisition probe directly invokes `SynthEnumerate` with five supplied grammars. Linear, quadratic, hinge, and absolute-value maps transfer exactly on all 20 acquired cells; the three grammars omitting the needed `Times`, `Max`, or `Abs` operator refuse 15/15 cells at the size boundary. Median 100-call native application cost is 4.70–5.72 µs. This measures caller-supplied grammar acquisition and exact-term reuse only; it does not exercise residual routing, nonlinear DimensionGenerator induction, or mint/adoption ([`SUBSTRATE_SYNTHENUMERATE_NONLINEAR_MAP_V1_1_RESULTS_20260929.md`](SUBSTRATE_SYNTHENUMERATE_NONLINEAR_MAP_V1_1_RESULTS_20260929.md), [preregistration](SUBSTRATE_SYNTHENUMERATE_NONLINEAR_MAP_V1_PREREG_20260929.md), [amendment](SUBSTRATE_SYNTHENUMERATE_NONLINEAR_MAP_V1_1_AMENDMENT_20260929.md), [analysis](../../../evaluation/reports/synthenumerate_nonlinear_map_v1_1_20260929_analysis.json), [raw](../../../evaluation/reports/synthenumerate_nonlinear_map_v1_1_20260929_raw.jsonl), [comparison audit](../../../evaluation/reports/synthenumerate_nonlinear_map_capture_comparison_v1_20260929.json)). The first current-source end-to-end Rung 3 nonlinear-map panel reaches graph-authored enumeration in 20/20 fresh workers. Tuple-correct v1.1.1 accepts/transfers the linear and quadratic maps 10/10; it also accepts one threshold-predicate hinge candidate that fails all 30 holdout predictions, and gaps on all five absolute-value cases at the 200,000-candidate limit. Source/capture/route gates pass; the semantic all-accepted-transfer gate fails (9/10 total). A prior v1 all-gap capture was diagnosed as list/tuple input mis-encoding; v1.1 had accepted workers lost to a `NodeID` serializer defect. End-to-end `pump` wall time is ~1.2 s on acquired maps, including adapter/rule work. This is actual Program-route evidence with a false-generalization counterexample and bounded-search gap, not `DimensionGenerator` mint/adoption ([`SUBSTRATE_SYNTHESIS_RUNG3_NONLINEAR_MAP_V1_1_1_RESULTS_20260929.md`](SUBSTRATE_SYNTHESIS_RUNG3_NONLINEAR_MAP_V1_1_1_RESULTS_20260929.md), [analysis](../../../evaluation/reports/synthesis_rung3_nonlinear_map_v1_1_1_20260929_analysis.json), [raw](../../../evaluation/reports/synthesis_rung3_nonlinear_map_v1_1_1_20260929_raw.jsonl), [lock](../../../evaluation/substrate_capability/synthesis_rung3_nonlinear_map_v1_1_1_20260929_lock.json)). A fresh paired balanced-hinge experiment isolates grammar breadth: the default route gaps at 200,000 candidates in 5/5 workers, while an oracle-injected `Plus/Minus/Max/TupleOf` inventory accepts and transfers exactly in 5/5 (30/30 holdout points), with 61,384 candidates and 650 ms median end-to-end `pump` time versus the default cap and 1,804 ms. All eight integrity gates and all three contrast thresholds pass. The intervention supplies the correct operators manually; automatic residual-to-grammar selection remains unmeasured ([results](SUBSTRATE_SYNTHESIS_RUNG3_HINGE_GRAMMAR_CONTRAST_V1_RESULTS_20260929.md), [protocol](SUBSTRATE_SYNTHESIS_RUNG3_HINGE_GRAMMAR_CONTRAST_V1_PREREG_20260929.md), [analysis](../../../evaluation/reports/synthesis_rung3_hinge_grammar_contrast_v1_20260929_analysis.json), [raw](../../../evaluation/reports/synthesis_rung3_hinge_grammar_contrast_v1_20260929_raw.jsonl), [lock](../../../evaluation/substrate_capability/synthesis_rung3_hinge_grammar_contrast_v1_20260929_lock.json)). A preregistered current-source selector-transfer repeat tests two scalar piecewise-scaling parameterizations. Without harness operator injection, the graph rule chooses `thresholded_greater_branch` and `Times/Gt/IfThenElse` in 10/10 trigger-wording workers; all 60 holdout predictions are exact. Neutral lexical ablation chooses no shape and hits the 200,000-candidate ceiling in 10/10 workers. Candidate counts fall 1.90–1.94×, though full pump wall time does not consistently improve. This is transfer of one authored selector shape, not learned grammar induction or broad language understanding ([results](SUBSTRATE_SYNTHESIS_OPERATOR_SHAPE_TRANSFER_V1_1_1_RESULTS_20260929.md), [protocol](SUBSTRATE_SYNTHESIS_OPERATOR_SHAPE_TRANSFER_V1_PREREG_20260929.md), [amendments](SUBSTRATE_SYNTHESIS_OPERATOR_SHAPE_TRANSFER_V1_1_AMENDMENT_20260929.md), [final capture amendment](SUBSTRATE_SYNTHESIS_OPERATOR_SHAPE_TRANSFER_V1_1_1_AMENDMENT_20260929.md), [analysis](../../../evaluation/reports/synthesis_operator_shape_transfer_v1_1_1_20260929_analysis.json), [raw](../../../evaluation/reports/synthesis_operator_shape_transfer_v1_1_1_20260929_raw.jsonl), [lock](../../../evaluation/substrate_capability/synthesis_operator_shape_transfer_v1_1_1_20260929_lock.json)). The corresponding `thresholded_lower_branch` selector is also measured on two new scalar piecewise tasks. Trigger wording selects `Times/Lt/IfThenElse` and transfers exactly in 10/10 workers (60/60 holdout points); neutral wording selects no shape and caps out in 10/10. Candidate reduction is 3.96× for threshold 4 and 1.44× for threshold 6, so the preregistered ≥1.5× reduction gate fails narrowly for the latter despite exact transfer. This confirms a second authored selector family while exposing task-dependent enumeration cost; it is not novel grammar learning ([results](SUBSTRATE_SYNTHESIS_LOWER_SHAPE_TRANSFER_V1_RESULTS_20260929.md), [protocol](SUBSTRATE_SYNTHESIS_LOWER_SHAPE_TRANSFER_V1_PREREG_20260929.md), [analysis](../../../evaluation/reports/synthesis_lower_shape_transfer_v1_20260929_analysis.json), [raw](../../../evaluation/reports/synthesis_lower_shape_transfer_v1_20260929_raw.jsonl), [lock](../../../evaluation/substrate_capability/synthesis_lower_shape_transfer_v1_20260929_lock.json)). A third scalar task family, clamp composition `Min(upper,Max(x,lower))`, succeeds through the default grammar with no operator-shape selection or injected operator list. Bounds `[0,5]` and `[-2,4]` each accept/transfer 5/5 fresh runs across 30/30 holdout points; median work is 8,230 and 6,663 candidates, with 27–34 ms median end-to-end `pump` wall time. All nine integrity and both transfer gates pass. This supports small fixed-ontology composition outside the two authored threshold shapes, not broad default-grammar tractability or new operator learning ([results](SUBSTRATE_SYNTHESIS_UNSHAPED_CLAMP_TRANSFER_V1_RESULTS_20260929.md), [protocol](SUBSTRATE_SYNTHESIS_UNSHAPED_CLAMP_TRANSFER_V1_PREREG_20260929.md), [analysis](../../../evaluation/reports/synthesis_unshaped_clamp_transfer_v1_20260929_analysis.json), [raw](../../../evaluation/reports/synthesis_unshaped_clamp_transfer_v1_20260929_raw.jsonl), [lock](../../../evaluation/substrate_capability/synthesis_unshaped_clamp_transfer_v1_20260929_lock.json)). A separate source-locked candidate-snapshot route accepts one Rung 3 Program/IR on each of two clamp tasks and preserves exact outputs on 60/60 held-outs after both eager and shared-graph-page forks; all 15 checks pass. The frozen and examples-ingested/no-pump controls contain no task Program. This is evidence for branch-safe execution of synthesized structure, not broad synthesis or CoW performance ([results](SUBSTRATE_PROGRAM_IR_CANDIDATE_SNAPSHOT_TRANSFER_V1_RESULTS_20260930.md), [protocol](SUBSTRATE_PROGRAM_IR_CANDIDATE_SNAPSHOT_V1_PREREG_20260930.md), [analysis](../../../evaluation/reports/program_ir_candidate_snapshot_transfer_v1_analysis_20260930.json), [raw](../../../evaluation/reports/program_ir_candidate_snapshot_transfer_v1_20260930_raw.json), [lock](../../../evaluation/substrate_capability/program_ir_candidate_snapshot_transfer_v1_20260930_lock.json)). A locked ambiguity replication shows sparse/default accepts the threshold shortcut and fails all 30 held-outs; one known counterexample moves broad search to a cap-limited gap (5/5), while a supplied compact grammar transfers exactly (30/30, 5/5). The fixed query and grammar are experimenter-supplied, so no autonomous ambiguity resolution is established ([results](SUBSTRATE_SYNTHESIS_AMBIGUITY_COUNTEREXAMPLE_V1_RESULTS_20260930.md), [protocol](SUBSTRATE_SYNTHESIS_AMBIGUITY_COUNTEREXAMPLE_V1_PREREG_20260930.md), [analysis](../../../evaluation/reports/synthesis_ambiguity_counterexample_v1_analysis_20260930.json), [raw](../../../evaluation/reports/synthesis_ambiguity_counterexample_v1_20260930_raw.json), [lock](../../../evaluation/substrate_capability/synthesis_ambiguity_counterexample_v1_20260930_lock.json)). A source-locked five-block generated-pool versus MDL-resynthesis study spans triangular number, square, and Euler tasks; all 60 workers pass held-out correctness, but complete-pool entropy takes about 6x/77x/32x more charged learner wall than the single-survivor resynthesis control while reducing query labels. This negative efficiency result is bounded to three authored functions and an inline matched-grammar CEGIS replay, not production-API integration ([results](SUBSTRATE_GENERATED_POOL_CEGIS_COMPARISON_V1_5_RESULTS_20261001.md), [preregistration](SUBSTRATE_GENERATED_POOL_CEGIS_COMPARISON_V1_PREREG_20261001.md), [analysis](../../../evaluation/reports/substrate_generated_pool_cegis_comparison_v1_5_20261001_analysis.json), [raw capture](../../../evaluation/reports/substrate_generated_pool_cegis_comparison_v1_5_20261001_raw.json), [lock](../../../evaluation/substrate_capability/substrate_generated_pool_cegis_comparison_v1_5_20261001_lock.json)). A 120-worker source-locked grammar-size × probe-diversity ladder completes without worker errors/timeouts and shows sharply rising generated-pool cost in the larger triangular/Euler cells; added probes also enlarge behavioral pools and improve target retention in selected cells. This is a two-repeat, three-authored-task scale signal, not a natural-task law, and its RSS deltas are too coarse for memory claims ([scale results](SUBSTRATE_GENERATED_POOL_SCALE_V1_RESULTS_20261001.md), [preregistration](SUBSTRATE_GENERATED_POOL_SCALE_V1_PREREG_20261001.md), [analysis](../../../evaluation/reports/substrate_generated_pool_scale_v1_20261001_analysis.json), [raw](../../../evaluation/reports/substrate_generated_pool_scale_v1_20261001_raw.json)). A source-locked 120-cell eager/streaming generator comparison preserves exact ordered-pool and held-out-support parity in every pair; streaming lowers median Python-traced peak allocation by 2.59× but shows no wall-time gain (median eager/streaming 1.014×). This is opt-in, native allocations are not measured, and tracing affects absolute timing ([results](SUBSTRATE_GENERATOR_STREAMING_PROBE_V1_RESULTS_20261001.md), [protocol](SUBSTRATE_GENERATOR_STREAMING_PROBE_V1_PREREG_20261001.md), [analysis](../../../evaluation/reports/substrate_generator_streaming_probe_v1_20261001_analysis.json), [raw](../../../evaluation/reports/substrate_generator_streaming_probe_v1_20261001_raw.json)). A second source-locked 12-pair panel adds `Lt`, `Gt`, `Eq`, and `IfThenElse`; all bounded pools complete with exact eager/streaming ordered-pool and held-out parity. Streaming reduces Python-traced peak allocation by 1.09×–3.83× across grouped cells, while two-repeat timing remains near parity. This exercises ternary cohort construction only; native allocations and new task-family transfer remain unmeasured ([results](SUBSTRATE_GENERATOR_STREAMING_GRAMMAR_V1_RESULTS_20261001.md), [protocol](SUBSTRATE_GENERATOR_STREAMING_GRAMMAR_V1_PREREG_20261001.md), [analysis](../../../evaluation/reports/substrate_generator_streaming_grammar_v1_20261001_analysis.json), [raw](../../../evaluation/reports/substrate_generator_streaming_grammar_v1_20261001_raw.json)). A 15-worker off-support ambiguity v1.1 replication passes 10/10 analyzer gates: default enumeration accepts identity on examples also compatible with square (0/20 analytic square holdouts), transfers square exactly when examples disambiguate it (20/20), and gaps on contradiction only at the 200k candidate cap (5/5). This measures predictive underdetermination and cap cost in one scalar grammar, not intended-rule identification or a general query policy ([results](SUBSTRATE_SYNTHESIS_OFFSUPPORT_AMBIGUITY_V1_1_RESULTS_20261001.md), [protocol](SUBSTRATE_SYNTHESIS_OFFSUPPORT_AMBIGUITY_V1_PREREG_20261001.md), [amendment](SUBSTRATE_SYNTHESIS_OFFSUPPORT_AMBIGUITY_V1_1_AMENDMENT_20261001.md), [runner](../../../scripts/substrate_synthesis_offsupport_ambiguity_v1_1.py), [analyzer](../../../scripts/analyze_substrate_synthesis_offsupport_ambiguity_v1_1.py), [lock](../../../evaluation/substrate_capability/synthesis_offsupport_ambiguity_v1_1_20261001_lock.json), [raw](../../../evaluation/reports/synthesis_offsupport_ambiguity_v1_1_20261001_raw.jsonl), [analysis](../../../evaluation/reports/synthesis_offsupport_ambiguity_v1_1_20261001_analysis.json)). A locked ten-worker grammar-coverage probe finds no fitting Program through size 5 with `Div` omitted (5/5 complete bounded searches), then recovers `x / 2` after experimenter-supplied grammar expansion, transferring exactly to 35/35 odd/negative holdouts; this is held-operator application, not primitive invention ([results](SUBSTRATE_SYNTHESIS_DECLARED_GRAMMAR_EXPANSION_V1_RESULTS_20261001.md), [protocol](SUBSTRATE_SYNTHESIS_DECLARED_GRAMMAR_EXPANSION_V1_PREREG_20261001.md), [runner](../../../scripts/substrate_synthesis_declared_grammar_expansion_v1.py), [analyzer](../../../scripts/analyze_substrate_synthesis_declared_grammar_expansion_v1.py), [lock](../../../evaluation/substrate_capability/synthesis_declared_grammar_expansion_v1_20261001_lock.json), [raw](../../../evaluation/reports/synthesis_declared_grammar_expansion_v1_20261001_raw.json), [analysis](../../../evaluation/reports/synthesis_declared_grammar_expansion_v1_20261001_analysis.json)). A ten-worker source-locked escalation panel on the standalone `domains.generator.synthesize` helper recovers division after the caller supplies `Div` (35/35 held-outs); `x % 3` is a bounded no-fit only for that helper because its three supplied grammars omit `Mod` (all size-5 stages complete, 215 ms median total). This does not describe the production Rust grammar; see the [scope amendment](SUBSTRATE_SYNTHESIS_GRAMMAR_ESCALATION_V1_SCOPE_AMENDMENT_20261001.md) ([results](SUBSTRATE_SYNTHESIS_GRAMMAR_ESCALATION_V1_RESULTS_20261001.md), [protocol](SUBSTRATE_SYNTHESIS_GRAMMAR_ESCALATION_V1_PREREG_20261001.md), [runner](../../../scripts/substrate_synthesis_grammar_escalation_v1.py), [analyzer](../../../scripts/analyze_substrate_synthesis_grammar_escalation_v1.py), [lock](../../../evaluation/substrate_capability/synthesis_grammar_escalation_v1_20261001_lock.json), [raw](../../../evaluation/reports/synthesis_grammar_escalation_v1_20261001_raw.json), [analysis](../../../evaluation/reports/synthesis_grammar_escalation_v1_20261001_analysis.json)). A separate production-path Gamma panel confirms Γ supplies a finite operator set including `Mod` without explicit caller ops: native synthesis selects `value % 2` and transfers exactly to 40/40 held-outs. Factorial, absent from the native operator table, gaps at 200,000 candidates in 5/5 workers. This is graph-authored selection among held operators, not primitive invention ([results](SUBSTRATE_PRODUCTION_GAMMA_OPERATOR_SELECTION_V1_RESULTS_20261001.md), [protocol](SUBSTRATE_PRODUCTION_GAMMA_OPERATOR_SELECTION_V1_PREREG_20261001.md), [runner](../../../scripts/substrate_production_gamma_operator_selection_v1.py), [analyzer](../../../scripts/analyze_substrate_production_gamma_operator_selection_v1.py), [lock](../../../evaluation/substrate_capability/production_gamma_operator_selection_v1_20261001_lock.json), [raw](../../../evaluation/reports/production_gamma_operator_selection_v1_20261001_raw.json), [analysis](../../../evaluation/reports/production_gamma_operator_selection_v1_20261001_analysis.json)). A source-locked three-process full-seed `WorldAdapter` panel then wired an independent adequacy receipt to the actual write-shape `teach()` boundary: five refused evidence states preserved codebook shapes/epoch, and one accepted receipt adopted exactly once per worker. This is an opt-in consistency gate over harness-supplied labels, not authenticated oracle evidence or general induction ([results](SUBSTRATE_ADEQUACY_RECEIPT_ADOPTION_V1_RESULTS_20261002.md), [protocol](SUBSTRATE_ADEQUACY_RECEIPT_ADOPTION_V1_PREREG_20261002.md), [amendment](SUBSTRATE_ADEQUACY_RECEIPT_ADOPTION_V1_1_AMENDMENT_20261002.md), [runner](../../../evaluation/substrate_capability/substrate_adequacy_receipt_adoption_v1.py), [analysis](../../../evaluation/reports/substrate_adequacy_receipt_adoption_v1_analysis_20261002.json)). A three-pair writer/reader subprocess panel then persists the accepted code-write shape, restores exact attributes after full-seed restart, and confirms same-receipt replay is idempotent while source/request changes are refused. Its initial replay capture found duplicate live nodes; the `teach()` exact-attribute reuse fix passes the amended 18-check panel. This is one authored example and replayable caller-declared evidence, not authenticated provenance ([persistence results](SUBSTRATE_ADEQUACY_RECEIPT_PERSISTENCE_V1_RESULTS_20261002.md), [amendment](SUBSTRATE_ADEQUACY_RECEIPT_PERSISTENCE_V1_1_AMENDMENT_20261002.md), [diagnostic](../../../evaluation/reports/substrate_adequacy_receipt_persistence_v1_initial_diagnostic_20261002.json), [analysis](../../../evaluation/reports/substrate_adequacy_receipt_persistence_v1_analysis_20261002.json)). A follow-up profile found that persistence initially serialized the entire native rule store just to check one commitment gate; prefix-filtered export now keeps the confirmed persisted-teach path at 59.5 ms median, while the direct full-versus-prefix export timings remain order-sensitive. The previous multi-second writer observation came from a semantically failed replay capture and is retained only as diagnostic context ([persistence results](SUBSTRATE_ADEQUACY_RECEIPT_PERSISTENCE_V1_RESULTS_20261002.md), [performance amendment](SUBSTRATE_ADEQUACY_RECEIPT_PERSISTENCE_V1_2_AMENDMENT_20261002.md), [analysis](../../../evaluation/reports/substrate_adequacy_receipt_persistence_v1_analysis_20261002.json)). A three-worker fresh-process integration panel now applies that receipt gate to parity and clamp acquisition and an identity-as-square counterexample. It records source-hashed oracle labels, obtains exact native/Python transfer on 183 held-outs, and refuses the false singleton without codebook growth. The oracle is separately hashed and recomputed but remains harness-authored, not an authenticated authority ([results](SUBSTRATE_INDEPENDENT_ADEQUACY_CODEWRITE_TRANSFER_V1_RESULTS_20261002.md), [protocol](SUBSTRATE_INDEPENDENT_ADEQUACY_CODEWRITE_TRANSFER_V1_PREREG_20261002.md), [analysis](../../../evaluation/reports/substrate_independent_adequacy_codewrite_transfer_v1_analysis_20261002.json)). A paired finite-domain coverage study then shows the receipt's boundary directly: identical audit evidence accepts an identity-as-square false candidate when its only fresh probe is x=1, but rejects it when the domain includes distinguishing points; ungated controls adopt and fail held-outs in both conditions. This measures conditional guard utility and a designed coverage failure, not natural prevalence ([results](SUBSTRATE_ADEQUACY_PROBE_COVERAGE_AB_V1_RESULTS_20261002.md), [protocol](SUBSTRATE_ADEQUACY_PROBE_COVERAGE_AB_V1_PREREG_20261002.md), [analysis](../../../evaluation/reports/substrate_adequacy_probe_coverage_ab_v1_analysis_20261002.json)). The cross-family adoption-boundary panel passes 8/8 checks across three fresh workers each on parity, clamp, and square; false evidence is refused and truthful evidence preserves the exact two-node, one-Agent-field adoption delta on all nine workers. This is a transaction-boundary result, not a full-conversation or general induction claim ([results](SUBSTRATE_ADEQUACY_ADOPTION_BOUNDARY_V2_RESULTS_20261002.md), [protocol](SUBSTRATE_ADEQUACY_ADOPTION_BOUNDARY_V2_PREREG_20261002.md), [raw](../../../evaluation/reports/substrate_adequacy_adoption_boundary_v2_raw_20261002.json), [analysis](../../../evaluation/reports/substrate_adequacy_adoption_boundary_v2_analysis_20261002.json)). A matched cold/warmed full `converse` panel then passes 8/8 checks across 18 workers: truthful evidence adopts and missing evidence refuses across the same three families. Warmed target wall medians are 111–116 ms, while aggregate process CPU medians are 1.65–1.70 s; this is not yet attributed to individual rules/threads and is not a per-core cost ([results](SUBSTRATE_ADEQUACY_WARM_CONVERSE_COST_V1_RESULTS_20261002.md), [protocol](SUBSTRATE_ADEQUACY_WARM_CONVERSE_COST_V1_PREREG_20261002.md), [order amendment](SUBSTRATE_ADEQUACY_WARM_CONVERSE_COST_V1_1_AMENDMENT_20261002.md), [analysis](../../../evaluation/reports/substrate_adequacy_warm_converse_cost_v1_analysis_20261002.json)).
